# 2026-10-10 Г?" FIX-154 trusted higher-timeframe BUY assessment (implementation)

- **Problem:** every bearish 1h row had equal veto authority, including
  historical rows with unknown data-quality provenance; there was no durable
  explanation of the effective 5m/1h BUY context.
- **Root cause:** BUY validation and consolidation read raw `TradeSignal`
  decisions directly, with no assessment of quality or bearish strength.
- **Solution:** added `data_quality_valid` persistence to `TradeSignal` and
  `AnalysisService`; introduced `HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService`; wired it
  into direct BUY, accumulated-evidence authority, consolidation and
  observational progressive-add evidence. The SOFT 1h result is capped at 25%
  and uses immutable effective decisions; HARD and unknown bearish evidence
  vetoes. `V107` creates `data_quality_valid` and append-only
  `buy_htf_assessment_audit`.
- **Files/classes:** `TradeSignal`, `AnalysisService`,
  `HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService`, `TradeExecutionValidationService`,
  `OpportunityConsolidationService`, `ExecutionIntelligenceService`,
  `BuyHtfAssessmentAuditStore`, `V107__fix_154_trusted_higher_timeframe_buy.sql`,
  and focused tests.
- **Database impact:** new nullable `trade_signal.data_quality_valid`; no
  backfill. Historical `NULL` stays unknown. New append-only audit table.
- **Production / Replay impact:** the same shared validation service is used
  for both. Audit failure is explicitly non-blocking. V108 ships because live
  runtime outcome writers require its schema; reconciliation remains disabled.
- **Validation status:** focused Maven tests could not run in this shell because
  the active JDK reports `release version 21 not supported`; execute with the
  project Java 21 toolchain before release.

The shell was switched to the installed Java 21 toolchain
(`C:\Program Files\Java\jdk-21`). Focused Maven verification then passed:
`HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentServiceTest`,
`TradeExecutionValidationServiceTest`, and
`OpportunityConsolidationServiceTest`.

## FIX-154 review-round correction Г?" 2026-10-10

- **Replay parity:** V107 now brings `trade_signal_test` forward with the
  V97 BTC freshness fields, V106 `signal_mode`, and FIX-154
  `data_quality_valid`, matching the reflection-based Replay writer.
- **Audit isolation:** audit records are snapshot and submitted to a bounded,
  one-thread executor. Replay never writes a Production audit row, and audit
  saturation/failure only increments an in-memory metric.
- **Evidence accuracy:** audit candle close times are derived from candle open
  time plus the interval, hard-confirmation is persisted, and both legacy veto
  codes are retained.
- **Startup safety:** outcome-schema verification now occurs at bean creation;
  FIX-154 verifies its required production and replay columns before runtime
  admission.
- **Validation:** Java 21 focused tests passed, including the higher-timeframe
  matrix, validation, consolidation and early outcome-schema verifier tests.

  sizing, neutral-5m non-escalation, untrusted non-bearish context, quality
  boundaries, and Replay audit suppression.

  Replay-table/FIX-154 audit schema creation when the MySQL release test stage
  is run.
- Added read-only FIX-154 health counters at `/api/fix154/health`.

and the remaining external MySQL/release proof.

## FIX-153 automatic reconciliation enabled Г?" 2026-10-10

- Problem: a missing historical candle left current candle ingestion healthy
  but blocked later signal generation for the affected lane.
- Root cause: the data-quality gate correctly rejects incomplete windows; the
  recovery component was present but disabled.
- Solution: enabled OFF-mode token-free reconciliation, kept its one-worker
  executor as the default, and added deterministic tests for reconnect
  evidence, bad local close-time replacement, and per-pass REST budgeting.
- Files changed: `application.yml`, `LocalCandleReconciliationConfig`, and
  `LocalCandleReconciliationServiceTest`.
- Database impact: uses existing V108 audit/outcome tables; no data rewrite or
  backfill was executed by this change.
- Production/Replay impact: recovery is non-executable and has no Replay
  authority. Targeted Java 21 reconciliation tests passed; real-MySQL release
  tests still require the disposable test environment.

## Flyway V107 checksum recovery Г?" 2026-10-10

- Problem: startup failed because the V107 file was changed after that version
  had been applied to the local database.
- Solution: restored the exact applied V107 content and checksum
  (`-501147284`). Moved the later Replay-table columns and audit
  `hard_confirmation_present` column into forward-only V109.
- Verification: Flyway's checksum calculator matches the stored V107 value;
  `Fix154MigrationContractTest` and `LocalCandleReconciliationServiceTest`
  pass on Java 21.
# Trade Investigation Agent

## Role

Investigate one Crypto AI Trader trade from durable evidence. Explain what
happened, why the system entered and exited, whether it followed the intended
rules, and whether the entry/exit was supported by the information available
at that time.

Investigations are read-only. Do not change code, configuration, services,
database records, review states, Replay work, or deployment state.

## Required input

Obtain or resolve:

- Environment and application: local, test, or production.
- Database host, port, and schema.
- One trade reference: `paper_position.id`, `wallet_trade.id`, or a symbol plus
  a bounded approximate entry-time window.
- Optional question or suspected issue.

Before analysis, confirm the symbol, trade/position ID, running version or FIX
level, and ingestion mode. Read the actual schema before choosing tables or
columns. Do not assume a table is in `crypto_ai` when it may belong to
`crypto_ai_v2`.

## Evidence rules

- Use configured database and SSH/log connections without displaying secrets.
- Use bounded, read-only queries and targeted log searches.
- Treat stored timestamps as UTC; display both UTC and KSA (`UTC+03:00`).
- Attach record IDs and timestamped log evidence to every factual conclusion.
- Distinguish **confirmed fact**, **supported inference**, and **missing or
  conflicting evidence**.
- Missing logs or records do not prove that no execution/effect occurred.
- A saved signal does not prove execution. A callback return does not prove a
  transaction committed. Under FIX-153, `market_price_event.APPLIED` proves a
  committed successful protection callback, but can be a no-op when there was
  no open position; find the position/trade mutation separately.
- Current configuration does not prove which settings were active at entry. If
  no configuration history exists, report that limitation.

## Investigation workflow

### 1. Resolve identity and environment

Link the trade reference to, where available:

- `paper_position`
- `wallet_trade`
- entry and exit `trade_signal`
- `signal_processing_work`
- wallet/position execution records

Record entry and exit times in UTC and KSA. Confirm whether the trader ran in
local-candle OFF mode or a legacy shared-market mode.

### 2. Collect a small evidence bundle

Start with exact IDs and a narrow time window. Expand only when the evidence
requires it. Collect:

| Evidence | Establishes |
| --- | --- |
| `paper_position` | lifecycle, entry/exit, stop, target, status |
| `wallet_trade` | executed quantity, prices, fees, linked signal |
| `trade_signal` | decision, strategy, scores, entry eligibility, reasons |
| `signal_processing_work` | claim, attempt, completion, expiry, uncertainty |
| `candle` / `technical_indicator` | market data and analysis inputs available then |
| `market_price_event` | intra-candle protection observations and outcomes |
| configuration/version evidence | applicable rules, when historically available |
| application and timing logs | analysis, execution, protection, exceptions, waits |

Avoid fetching large signal explanation fields until they are needed.

### 3. Reconstruct the timeline

Build a timeline, explicitly showing missing stages:

`candle closed Г+' signal saved Г+' work claimed Г+' locks acquired Г+' entry committed Г+' protection observations Г+' exit decision Г+' exit committed`

Measure separately:

- candle close to signal generation;
- signal generation to processing/entry;
- observation time to protection completion;
- exit-trigger evidence to recorded exit.

Correlate delays only with logs/events for the same symbol, position, signal,
or price-event identity. A nearby slow SQL query is not proof of causation.

### 4. Assess entry correctness and quality

Use only evidence that was available at entry. Check:

- signal mode, closed-candle identity, freshness, and data quality;
- final decision and entry-allowed result;
- BTC context, higher-timeframe, ATR, liquidity, risk/reward, sizing, and
  strategy gates that applied at that time;
- correct signal/position linkage and absence of duplicate/stale-retry entry;
- quantity, wallet balance, risk limits, fees, and fill/reference-price gap.

Return two separate conclusions:

- **Execution correctness:** followed or violated the implemented rules.
- **Entry quality:** supported, weak, or unverifiable from contemporaneous
  evidence.

Subsequent price movement may evaluate the outcome; it must never be presented
as information the entry decision had.

### 5. Reconstruct protection and stop-loss behavior

For FIX-153, inspect accepted `LEGACY`/`APPLIED` price events and retain
`PENDING`, `NON_CANONICAL`, and `PROTECTION_NOT_APPLIED` records as evidence
gaps or exclusions. Never substitute candle OHLC for absent intra-candle price
evidence.

For each relevant accepted observation:

1. Determine the then-active stop, target, trailing stop, and profit-lock state.
2. Find the first accepted observation that met an exit threshold.
3. Confirm that protection evaluated it and identify the resulting position or
   wallet execution record.
4. Explain delays, rejections, stale/out-of-order classification, exceptions,
   lock waits, or pool pressure.

Classify the exit path accurately: fixed stop-loss, trailing stop, profit-lock,
take-profit, signal-driven exit, manual exit, or unknown.

### 6. Assess exit correctness and quality

Compare the recorded exit reason, quantity, and price with trigger evidence.
Check threshold reach, accepted-observation identity, protection timing, and
the committed execution. Calculate maximum favourable/adverse movement during
the holding period separately from the contemporaneous exit assessment.

### 7. Reconcile financial results

Recalculate using execution records:

- bought/sold quantities and weighted entry/exit prices;
- gross P&L, fees, and net P&L;
- partial exits, remaining quantity, and realized versus unrealized values.

Flag discrepancies among wallet records, position records, and UI output.

### 8. Diagnose cause and use Replay carefully

Classify findings as one or more of:

- strategy decision;
- data freshness or coverage;
- analysis delay;
- execution failure;
- protection failure;
- database, lock, or connection-pool contention;
- accounting/UI discrepancy;
- insufficient evidence.

Run Replay only as a controlled comparison with equivalent candles, accepted
prices, strategy version, and settings. State every unavailable input or
material difference. Never claim parity by substituting candle OHLC for missing
price evidence or treating `PENDING` as accepted.

## Required final report

1. **Trade identity and environment** Г?" IDs, symbol, version/mode, scope.
2. **UTC/KSA timeline** Г?" evidence-backed sequence and latency breakdown.
3. **Entry** Г?" execution correctness and entry quality.
4. **Exit** Г?" execution correctness and exit quality.
5. **Stop-loss/profit-lock** Г?" worked, failed, or unverifiable, with evidence.
6. **P&L reconciliation** Г?" quantities, prices, fees, and discrepancies.
7. **Root cause and confidence** Г?" confirmed defect, valid loss, configuration
   issue, delay, or insufficient evidence.
8. **Evidence references** Г?" record IDs, query/log timestamps, code/config
   references.
9. **Missing evidence** Г?" exactly what prevents a stronger conclusion.
10. **Recommended correction and regression checks** Г?" only when justified.

A profitable trade can violate the rules, and a losing trade can be a correct
execution. State those independently.

package com.crypto.service;

import com.crypto.config.AnalysisScoringProperties;
import com.crypto.domain.Candle;
import com.crypto.dto.CandleDataQualityResult;
import com.crypto.repository.CandleRepository;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import org.springframework.data.domain.PageRequest;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;

import java.time.Duration;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Comparator;
import java.util.List;

@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class CandleDataQualityService {

    private final CandleRepository candleRepository;
    private final AnalysisScoringProperties properties;

    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public CandleDataQualityResult validate(String symbol, String intervalCode) {
        int minimum = properties.dataQuality().minimumCandles();
        List<Candle> candles = new ArrayList<>(candleRepository.findClosedCandles(
                symbol, intervalCode, PageRequest.of(0, minimum)
        ));
        candles.sort(Comparator.comparing(Candle::getOpenTime));

        List<String> warnings = new ArrayList<>();
        int invalid = 0;
        for (Candle candle : candles) {
            boolean invalidOhlc = candle.getOpenPrice() == null
                    || candle.getHighPrice() == null
                    || candle.getLowPrice() == null
                    || candle.getClosePrice() == null
                    || candle.getVolume() == null
                    || candle.getOpenPrice().signum() <= 0
                    || candle.getHighPrice().compareTo(candle.getLowPrice()) < 0
                    || candle.getClosePrice().compareTo(candle.getLowPrice()) < 0
                    || candle.getClosePrice().compareTo(candle.getHighPrice()) > 0
                    || candle.getVolume().signum() < 0;
            if (invalidOhlc) {
                invalid++;
            }
        }

        Duration expectedStep = intervalDuration(intervalCode);
        int missing = 0;
        if (!expectedStep.isZero()) {
            for (int index = 1; index < candles.size(); index++) {
                Duration actual = Duration.between(candles.get(index - 1).getOpenTime(), candles.get(index).getOpenTime());
                if (actual.compareTo(expectedStep) > 0) {
                    long steps = actual.toMillis() / expectedStep.toMillis();
                    missing += Math.max(0, (int) steps - 1);
                }
            }
        } else {
            warnings.add("Unsupported interval for gap validation: " + intervalCode);
        }

        if (candles.size() < minimum) {
            warnings.add("Insufficient history: " + candles.size() + "/" + minimum + " closed candles");
        }
        if (missing > 0) {
            warnings.add("Missing " + missing + " candle(s) in the analysis window");
        }
        if (invalid > 0) {
            warnings.add("Found " + invalid + " candle(s) with invalid OHLCV data");
        }

        boolean valid = candles.size() >= minimum
                && missing <= properties.dataQuality().maximumMissingCandles()
                && (!properties.dataQuality().blockOnInvalidOhlc() || invalid == 0);

        return new CandleDataQualityResult(valid, minimum, candles.size(), missing, invalid, List.copyOf(warnings));
    }

    private Duration intervalDuration(String intervalCode) {
        if (intervalCode == null || intervalCode.length() < 2) {
            return Duration.ZERO;
        }
        try {
            long amount = Long.parseLong(intervalCode.substring(0, intervalCode.length() - 1));
            char unit = intervalCode.charAt(intervalCode.length() - 1);
            return switch (unit) {
                case 'm' -> Duration.ofMinutes(amount);
                case 'h' -> Duration.ofHours(amount);
                case 'd' -> Duration.ofDays(amount);
                case 'w' -> Duration.ofDays(amount * 7);
                default -> Duration.ZERO;
            };
        } catch (NumberFormatException exception) {
            return Duration.ZERO;
        }
    }
}
        Long parityComparisonNs = null;
        try (var inputAudit = sharedMarketEnabled ? com.crypto.shared.CandleInputAudit.open(jdbcTemplate,auditTransactionManager,"REPLAY:"+runId) : null) {
            long stageStartedNs = System.nanoTime();
            Map<String, Object> run = jdbcTemplate.queryForMap(
                    "SELECT symbol, start_time, end_time FROM analysis_test_run WHERE id = ?", runId);
            String symbol = String.valueOf(run.get("symbol"));
            Instant start = toInstant(run.get("start_time"));
            Instant end = toInstant(run.get("end_time"));

            updateRun(runId, "RUNNING", 2, "Loading historical candles and signals", null);
            checkStopRequested(runId);

            Instant contextStart = start.minus(REPLAY_CONTEXT_WARMUP);
            List<Candle> oneMinuteCandles = candles(symbol, "1m", contextStart, end);
            List<Candle> fiveMinuteCandles = candles(symbol, "5m", contextStart, end);
            List<Candle> oneHourCandles = candles(symbol, "1h", contextStart, end);
            List<Candle> requestedOneMinuteCandles = candlesInExecutionWindow(oneMinuteCandles, "1m", start, end);
            List<Candle> requestedFiveMinuteCandles = candlesInExecutionWindow(fiveMinuteCandles, "5m", start, end);
            List<Candle> requestedOneHourCandles = candlesInExecutionWindow(oneHourCandles, "1h", start, end);

            String btcSymbol = btcContextProperties.referenceSymbol();
            boolean replayBtcContext = btcContextProperties.enabled() && !btcSymbol.equalsIgnoreCase(symbol);
            List<Candle> btcOneMinuteCandles = replayBtcContext ? candles(btcSymbol, "1m", contextStart, end) : List.of();
            List<Candle> btcFiveMinuteCandles = replayBtcContext ? candles(btcSymbol, "5m", contextStart, end) : List.of();
            List<Candle> btcOneHourCandles = replayBtcContext ? candles(btcSymbol, "1h", contextStart, end) : List.of();

            List<TradeSignal> sourceSignals = signalRepository
                    .findBySymbolAndGeneratedAtBetweenOrderByGeneratedAtAsc(symbol, start, end);

            jdbcTemplate.update("UPDATE analysis_test_run SET source_signal_count=? WHERE id=?",
                throw new IllegalStateException("Replay lineage invariant violated for " + key
                        + ": signalIds=" + previous + "," + signal.getId());
            }
        }
        log.info("FIX-11H dataset lineage: symbol={}, entries={}", symbol, lineage.size());
        return new ReplayDataset(candleMap, lineage);
    }

    private List<Candle> candles(String symbol, String interval, Instant start, Instant end) {
        return candleRepository.findBySymbolAndIntervalCodeAndOpenTimeBetweenOrderByOpenTimeAsc(
                symbol, interval, start, end);
    }

    private int verifyEventResolution(long runId, String symbol, String interval, List<Candle> candles,
                                      int progressStart, int progressEnd) {
        int resolved = 0;
        int size = candles.size();
        for (int i = 0; i < size; i++) {
            checkStopRequested(runId);
            Candle candle = candles.get(i);
            List<Candle> latestAtEvent = candleRepository.findClosedCandlesAtOrBefore(
                    symbol, interval, candle.getOpenTime(), PageRequest.of(0, 1));
            if (!latestAtEvent.isEmpty() && candle.getOpenTime().equals(latestAtEvent.get(0).getOpenTime())) {
                resolved++;
            }
            if (i % 25 == 0 && size > 0) {
                int p = progressStart + (int) Math.round((progressEnd - progressStart) * (i / (double) size));
                updateRun(runId, "RUNNING", p,

package com.crypto.service;

import com.crypto.config.AnalysisScoringProperties;
import com.crypto.domain.Candle;
import com.crypto.dto.CandleDataQualityResult;
import com.crypto.repository.CandleRepository;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import org.springframework.data.domain.PageRequest;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;

import java.time.Duration;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Comparator;
import java.util.List;

@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class CandleDataQualityService {

    private final CandleRepository candleRepository;
    private final AnalysisScoringProperties properties;

    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public CandleDataQualityResult validate(String symbol, String intervalCode) {
        int minimum = properties.dataQuality().minimumCandles();
        List<Candle> candles = new ArrayList<>(candleRepository.findClosedCandles(
                symbol, intervalCode, PageRequest.of(0, minimum)
        ));
        candles.sort(Comparator.comparing(Candle::getOpenTime));

        List<String> warnings = new ArrayList<>();
        int invalid = 0;
        for (Candle candle : candles) {
            boolean invalidOhlc = candle.getOpenPrice() == null
                    || candle.getHighPrice() == null
                    || candle.getLowPrice() == null
                    || candle.getClosePrice() == null
                    || candle.getVolume() == null
                    || candle.getOpenPrice().signum() <= 0
                    || candle.getHighPrice().compareTo(candle.getLowPrice()) < 0
                    || candle.getClosePrice().compareTo(candle.getLowPrice()) < 0
                    || candle.getClosePrice().compareTo(candle.getHighPrice()) > 0
                    || candle.getVolume().signum() < 0;
            if (invalidOhlc) {
                invalid++;
            }
        }

        Duration expectedStep = intervalDuration(intervalCode);
        int missing = 0;
        if (!expectedStep.isZero()) {
            for (int index = 1; index < candles.size(); index++) {
                Duration actual = Duration.between(candles.get(index - 1).getOpenTime(), candles.get(index).getOpenTime());
                if (actual.compareTo(expectedStep) > 0) {
                    long steps = actual.toMillis() / expectedStep.toMillis();
                    missing += Math.max(0, (int) steps - 1);
                }
            }
        } else {
            warnings.add("Unsupported interval for gap validation: " + intervalCode);
        }

        if (candles.size() < minimum) {
            warnings.add("Insufficient history: " + candles.size() + "/" + minimum + " closed candles");
        }
        if (missing > 0) {
            warnings.add("Missing " + missing + " candle(s) in the analysis window");
        }
        if (invalid > 0) {
            warnings.add("Found " + invalid + " candle(s) with invalid OHLCV data");
        }

        boolean valid = candles.size() >= minimum
                && missing <= properties.dataQuality().maximumMissingCandles()
                && (!properties.dataQuality().blockOnInvalidOhlc() || invalid == 0);

        return new CandleDataQualityResult(valid, minimum, candles.size(), missing, invalid, List.copyOf(warnings));
    }

    private Duration intervalDuration(String intervalCode) {
        if (intervalCode == null || intervalCode.length() < 2) {
            return Duration.ZERO;
        }
        try {
            long amount = Long.parseLong(intervalCode.substring(0, intervalCode.length() - 1));
            char unit = intervalCode.charAt(intervalCode.length() - 1);
            return switch (unit) {
                case 'm' -> Duration.ofMinutes(amount);
                case 'h' -> Duration.ofHours(amount);
                case 'd' -> Duration.ofDays(amount);
                case 'w' -> Duration.ofDays(amount * 7);
                default -> Duration.ZERO;
            };
        } catch (NumberFormatException exception) {
            return Duration.ZERO;
        }
    }
}

    private List<Candle> candles(String symbol, String interval, Instant start, Instant end) {
        return candleRepository.findBySymbolAndIntervalCodeAndOpenTimeBetweenOrderByOpenTimeAsc(
                symbol, interval, start, end);
    }

    private int verifyEventResolution(long runId, String symbol, String interval, List<Candle> candles,
                                      int progressStart, int progressEnd) {
        int resolved = 0;
        int size = candles.size();
        for (int i = 0; i < size; i++) {
            checkStopRequested(runId);
            Candle candle = candles.get(i);
            List<Candle> latestAtEvent = candleRepository.findClosedCandlesAtOrBefore(
                    symbol, interval, candle.getOpenTime(), PageRequest.of(0, 1));
            if (!latestAtEvent.isEmpty() && candle.getOpenTime().equals(latestAtEvent.get(0).getOpenTime())) {
                resolved++;
            }
            if (i % 25 == 0 && size > 0) {
                int p = progressStart + (int) Math.round((progressEnd - progressStart) * (i / (double) size));
                updateRun(runId, "RUNNING", p,
                        "Replaying " + interval + " candle " + (i + 1) + "/" + size, null);
            }
        }
        return resolved;
    }

    /** FIX-112A: Replay-local execution ledger keyed to the exact triggering signal. */
    public boolean entryConsumed(Long signalId) {
        return active() && signalId != null && required().consumedSignalIds.contains(signalId);
    }

    /** FIX-112A: record only after that exact shadow BUY (initial or add) executes. */
    public void markEntryConsumed(Long signalId) {
        if (signalId != null) required().consumedSignalIds.add(signalId);
    }

    /**
     * Adds a freshly generated replay signal to the as-of context stream. Signal generation
     * uses this incrementally so the production confluence/BTC services can resolve only
     * context that had genuinely been generated by the current replay timestamp.
     */
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class EntryConsumptionPolicy {
    private final WalletTradeRepository walletTradeRepository;
    private final ExecutionReplayScope replayScope;

    public EntryConsumptionState resolve(Long previousSignalId) {
        if (previousSignalId == null) return EntryConsumptionState.NOT_CONSUMED;
        if (replayScope.active()) {
            return replayScope.entryConsumed(previousSignalId)
                    ? EntryConsumptionState.CONSUMED
                    : EntryConsumptionState.NOT_CONSUMED;
        }
        // Production authority is the immutable executed BUY row keyed to the
        // exact triggering signal. This works for both first entries and adds.
        boolean executed = walletTradeRepository
                .findTopBySignalIdAndSideAndStatusOrderByExecutedAtDesc(
                        previousSignalId, "BUY", "EXECUTED")
                .isPresent();
        return executed ? EntryConsumptionState.CONSUMED : EntryConsumptionState.NOT_CONSUMED;
    }
}
public class TradeExecutionValidationService {

    private static final String EXECUTION_INTERVAL = "1m";
    private static final String CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL = "5m";
    private static final String TREND_INTERVAL = "1h";
    private static final Duration ONE_MINUTE_TRANSITION_MAX_AGE = Duration.ofMinutes(10);
    private static final Duration FIVE_MINUTE_MAX_AGE = Duration.ofMinutes(20);
    private static final Duration ONE_HOUR_MAX_AGE = Duration.ofHours(3);

    // FIX-11G: this mirrors the existing global CHASE_ENTRY_CUTOFF in
    // ExecutionIntelligenceService. We deliberately reuse the proven execution-quality
    // boundary instead of inventing a new confidence/quality threshold for this one route.
    // The downstream global Entry Quality guard still remains authoritative and re-checks it.
    private static final int BALANCED_TRANSITIONAL_MIN_ENTRY_QUALITY = 50;

    private final TradeSignalRepository signalRepository;
    private final WalletSettingsRepository settingsRepository;
    private final EntryConsumptionPolicy entryConsumptionPolicy;
    private final HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService htfAssessment;
    @Autowired(required = false)
    private ExecutionReplayScope replayScope;
    @Autowired(required = false)
    private BuyHtfAssessmentAuditStore htfAuditStore;
    @Autowired(required = false)
    private Fix154Metrics fix154Metrics;

    public ValidationResult validateBuy(TradeSignal executionSignal, int entryQualityScore) {
        ValidationResult structural = validateBaseSignal(executionSignal, true);
        if (!structural.allowed()) return structural;

        WalletSettings settings = settings();
        if (settings.isRequireNewBuyTransition()) {
            TradeSignal previous = previousSignal(executionSignal.getSymbol(), EXECUTION_INTERVAL, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt()).orElse(null);
            // FIX-112A: a bullish label alone is not a consumed entry. Only keep
            // BUY_CONTINUATION protection when that exact previous signal has an
            // executed BUY (Production) or executed shadow BUY (Replay).
            if (isFresh(previous, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt(), ONE_MINUTE_TRANSITION_MAX_AGE)
                    && isBullish(previous.getDecision())
                    && entryConsumptionPolicy.resolve(previous.getId()) != EntryConsumptionState.NOT_CONSUMED) {
                return ValidationResult.reject("BUY_CONTINUATION",
                        "BUY was not executed because a recent bullish opportunity for this symbol was already consumed. "
                                + "A new WATCH/NEUTRAL/SELL -> BUY transition is required.");
            }
        }

        TradeSignal fiveMinute = latestAtOrBefore(executionSignal, CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL);
        if (!isFresh(fiveMinute, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt(), FIVE_MINUTE_MAX_AGE)) {
            return ValidationResult.reject("MISSING_5M_CONFIRMATION",
                    "No fresh 5m confirmation was available for the 1m BUY signal.");
        }

        TradeSignal oneHour = latestAtOrBefore(executionSignal, TREND_INTERVAL);
        if (!isFresh(oneHour, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt(), ONE_HOUR_MAX_AGE)) {
            return ValidationResult.reject("MISSING_1H_CONTEXT",
                    "No fresh 1h strategic context was available for the 1m BUY signal.");
        }

        HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Assessment assessment=htfAssessment.assess(fiveMinute,true,oneHour,true);
        if(!assessment.allowed()) {
            recordAssessmentMetric(assessment);
            audit(executionSignal, "DIRECT_BUY", assessment, assessment.code(), "FIX-154 higher-timeframe assessment rejected this BUY.");
            return ValidationResult.reject(assessment.code(), "FIX-154 higher-timeframe assessment rejected this BUY.");
        }

        ExecutionProfile profile = ExecutionProfile.from(settings.getExecutionProfile());
        ValidationResult profileResult = switch (profile) {
            case CONSERVATIVE -> conservativeBuy(assessment.effectiveFiveMinute(), assessment.effectiveOneHour());
            // FIX-112B: only the fresh direct-BUY BALANCED path receives the narrow
            // 5m-NEUTRAL exception. validateBuyContext() and AGGRESSIVE fallback keep
            // calling balancedBuy() directly so their proven Production behavior is unchanged.
            case BALANCED -> balancedBuyWithNeutralFiveException(
                    executionSignal, assessment.effectiveFiveMinute(), assessment.effectiveOneHour(), entryQualityScore);
            case AGGRESSIVE -> aggressiveBuy(executionSignal, assessment.effectiveFiveMinute(), assessment.effectiveOneHour());
        // FIX-11M: Enable aggregate timing only for this Replay worker thread.
        // The shared AnalysisService decision path is unchanged; this is observation-only.
        analysisService.beginReplayAnalysisProfiling(runId);
        try (ExecutionReplayScope.Scope ignored = replayScope.open(runId, List.of(), o -> {})) {
        // FIX-043 Replay parity: fresh Replay has always generated technical snapshots/signals
        // chronologically for EVERY closed candle in the timeline. Production now restores the
        // same no-gap contract with asynchronous close-event processing plus chronological recovery.
        // Do not "optimize" Replay by sampling every Nth candle or by copying the production recovery
        // scheduler cadence; that would recreate the exact 1m->~5m blind-gap incident FIX-043 fixes.
        for (int index = 0; index < timeline.size(); index++) {
            checkStopRequested(runId);
            ReplayCandle replay = timeline.get(index);
            Candle candle = replay.candle();
            Instant evaluationTime = candle.getOpenTime().plusSeconds(intervalSeconds(replay.interval()));
            boolean inRequestedWindow = !evaluationTime.isBefore(executionStart) && !evaluationTime.isAfter(executionEnd);
            try {
                long operationStartedNs = System.nanoTime();
                java.util.Optional<IndicatorSnapshot> snapshot;
                try {
                    snapshot = dataSource == ReplayDataSource.DATASET
                            ? technicalIndicatorService.calculateSnapshotForRegression(
                                    replay.symbol(), replay.interval(), candle.getOpenTime(), replayDataset)
                            : technicalIndicatorService.calculateSnapshotForRegression(
                                    replay.symbol(), replay.interval(), candle.getOpenTime());
                } finally {
                    profiler.recordSnapshot(System.nanoTime() - operationStartedNs);
                }
                if (snapshot.isEmpty()) {
                    if (inRequestedWindow) {
                        errors++;
                        saveGenerationError(runId, replay.symbol(), replay.interval(), candle,
                                "Not enough historical candles to calculate the technical snapshot");
                    }
                    continue;
                }

                // Do not generate a signal whose candle would only have closed after the
                // requested test end. Warm-up rows before executionStart are intentionally
                // generated for as-of context, but are not counted or displayed as test output.
                if (evaluationTime.isAfter(executionEnd)) continue;

                replayScope.reference(evaluationTime);
                operationStartedNs = System.nanoTime();
                TradeSignal generated;
                try {
                    generated = analysisService.analyzeForRegression(snapshot.get(), evaluationTime);
                } finally {
                    profiler.recordAnalysis(System.nanoTime() - operationStartedNs);
                }
                replayScope.appendSignal(generated);
                SignalDecision decision = generated.getDecision();
                if (replay.primary()) generatedSignals.add(generated);

                if (replay.primary() && inRequestedWindow) {
                    if ("1m".equals(replay.interval())) { oneMinuteSignals++; if (isBuy(decision)) oneMinuteBuys++; }
                    else if ("5m".equals(replay.interval())) { fiveMinuteSignals++; if (isBuy(decision)) fiveMinuteBuys++; }
                    else if ("1h".equals(replay.interval())) { oneHourSignals++; if (isBuy(decision)) oneHourBuys++; }

                    if (decision == SignalDecision.BUY || decision == SignalDecision.STRONG_BUY) buys++;
                    else if (decision == SignalDecision.WATCH) watches++;
                    else if (decision == SignalDecision.SELL) sells++;
                    else if (decision == SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL) strongSells++;

                    operationStartedNs = System.nanoTime();
                    try {
                        jdbcTemplate.update("""
                            INSERT INTO analysis_test_signal
                                (test_run_id, source_signal_id, replay_generated, symbol, interval_code, candle_open_time,
                                 generated_at, latest_price, original_decision, final_decision,
                                 execution_effective_decision, total_score, confidence_score, raw_confidence_score,
                                 effective_confidence_score, primary_blocking_stage, detected_regime, candidate_regime,
                                 confirmed_regime, regime_candidate_count, entry_authority, entry_authority_max_position_percent,
                                 trend_score, volume_score, momentum_score, decision_authority_corrected, generation_error)
                            VALUES (?, NULL, 1, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 0, NULL)
                            """,
                            runId, replay.symbol(), replay.interval(), Timestamp.from(candle.getOpenTime()),
                            Timestamp.from(evaluationTime), generated.getLatestPrice(),
                            generated.getOriginalDecision() == null ? null : generated.getOriginalDecision().name(),
                            decision == null ? null : decision.name(),
                            decision == null ? null : decision.name(),
                            generated.getTotalScore(), generated.getConfidenceScore(), generated.getRawConfidenceScore(),
                            generated.getEffectiveConfidenceScore(), generated.getPrimaryBlockingStage(),
                            generated.getDetectedRegime() == null ? null : generated.getDetectedRegime().name(),
                            generated.getCandidateRegime() == null ? null : generated.getCandidateRegime().name(),
                            generated.getConfirmedRegime() == null ? null : generated.getConfirmedRegime().name(),
                            generated.getRegimeCandidateCount(), generated.getEntryAuthority(),
                            generated.getEntryAuthorityMaxPositionPercent(), generated.getTrendScore(),
                            generated.getVolumeScore(), generated.getMomentumScore());
                    } finally {
                        profiler.recordAnalysisTestSignalPersistence(System.nanoTime() - operationStartedNs);
                    }

                    // FIX-069: Persist the exact production TradeSignal shape as replay output.
                    // analysis_test_signal remains for backward-compatible diagnostics, while
                    // trade_signal_test is the canonical parity table used for field-by-field comparison.
                    // FIX-112D: Replay lineage uses exact market-candle identity, never
                    // generated_at or nearest-time matching. A legitimate Replay-only
                    // signal remains NULL. This metadata lookup cannot alter Production.
                    operationStartedNs = System.nanoTime();
                    Long sourceSignalId;
                    try {
                        sourceSignalId = dataSource == ReplayDataSource.DATASET
                                ? replayDataset.sourceSignalId(
                                        generated.getSymbol(), generated.getInterval(), generated.getCandleOpenTime())
                                : signalRepository.findBySymbolAndIntervalAndCandleOpenTime(
                                        generated.getSymbol(), generated.getInterval(), generated.getCandleOpenTime())
                                        .map(TradeSignal::getId).orElse(null);
                    } finally {
                        profiler.recordLineageLookup(System.nanoTime() - operationStartedNs);
                    }
                    operationStartedNs = System.nanoTime();
                    try {
                        persistTradeSignalTest(runId, generated, sourceSignalId, null);
                    } finally {
                        profiler.recordTradeSignalTestPersistence(System.nanoTime() - operationStartedNs);
                    }
                }
            } catch (ReplayCancellationException stop) {
                throw stop;
            } catch (Exception exception) {
                if (replay.primary() && inRequestedWindow) {
                    errors++;
                    saveGenerationError(runId, replay.symbol(), replay.interval(), candle, errorDetail(exception, 900));
                }
        jdbcTemplate.update("""
                INSERT INTO analysis_test_signal
                    (test_run_id, source_signal_id, replay_generated, symbol, interval_code, candle_open_time,
                     generated_at, latest_price, decision_authority_corrected, generation_error)
                VALUES (?, NULL, 1, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 0, ?)
                """, runId, symbol, interval, Timestamp.from(candle.getOpenTime()),
                Timestamp.from(candle.getOpenTime().plusSeconds(intervalSeconds(interval))),
                candle.getClosePrice(), error);
    }

    /**
     * FIX-069: write every field mapped by the TradeSignal JPA entity into trade_signal_test.
     * Reflection is intentional here: whenever production TradeSignal gains another @Column,
     * replay inherits it automatically instead of drifting behind a hand-maintained test schema.
     */
    private void persistTradeSignalTest(long runId, TradeSignal signal, Long sourceSignalId, String generationError) {
        try {
            java.util.List<String> columns = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
            java.util.List<Object> values = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
            columns.add("test_run_id"); values.add(runId);
            // FIX-112D / Replay=Production audit lineage: this ID points only to the
            // exact Production signal for the same symbol + interval + candle_open_time.
            // It is additive Replay metadata and is never an execution authority.
            columns.add("source_signal_id"); values.add(sourceSignalId);
            columns.add("replay_generated"); values.add(1);
            columns.add("generation_error"); values.add(generationError);

            for (Field field : TradeSignal.class.getDeclaredFields()) {
                if ("id".equals(field.getName()) || "createdAt".equals(field.getName())) continue;
                Column column = field.getAnnotation(Column.class);
                if (column == null) continue;
                field.setAccessible(true);
                Object value = field.get(signal);
                if (value instanceof Enum<?> e) value = e.name();
                if (value instanceof Instant instant) value = Timestamp.from(instant);
                String columnName = column.name();
                if (columnName == null || columnName.isBlank()) columnName = snakeCase(field.getName());
                columns.add(columnName);
                values.add(value);
            }

            String placeholders = String.join(",", java.util.Collections.nCopies(values.size(), "?"));
            jdbcTemplate.update("INSERT INTO trade_signal_test (" + String.join(",", columns) + ") VALUES (" + placeholders + ")", values.toArray());
        } catch (Exception e) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("Unable to persist production-shaped replay TradeSignal", e);
        }
    }

    private String snakeCase(String value) {
        return value.replaceAll("([a-z0-9])([A-Z])", "$1_$2").toLowerCase(java.util.Locale.ROOT);
    }

    private long intervalSeconds(String interval) {
        return switch (interval) {
            case "1m" -> 60L;
            case "5m" -> 300L;
            case "1h" -> 3600L;
            default -> 60L;
    @Transactional
    public TradeSignal analyzeRecovered(TechnicalIndicator indicator, Instant evaluationTime) {
        if (indicator == null) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Recovered technical indicator is required");
        }
        IndicatorSnapshot snapshot = toSnapshot(indicator);
        Instant asOf = evaluationTime == null ? snapshot.candleOpenTime() : evaluationTime;
        TradeSignal signal = buildSignal(snapshot, asOf, true, false);
        signal.setSignalMode("HISTORICAL_ONLY");
        return signalRepository.save(signal);
    }

    /**
     * Regression-only analysis path. Runs the same production scoring/final-decision
     * code but returns an unsaved TradeSignal so replay can never write to trade_signal.
     */
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public TradeSignal analyzeForRegression(IndicatorSnapshot snapshot, Instant evaluationTime) {
        if (snapshot == null) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Regression indicator snapshot is required");
        }
        ReplayAnalysisProfiler profiler = replayAnalysisProfiler.get();
        long startedNs = profiler == null ? 0L : System.nanoTime();
        try {
            return buildSignal(snapshot, evaluationTime == null ? snapshot.candleOpenTime() : evaluationTime, true, true);
        } finally {
            if (profiler != null) {
                profiler.recordCall(System.nanoTime() - startedNs);
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * FIX-11M: Called only by RegressionTestWorker around the fresh-signal stage.
     * This activates timing observation; it does not alter buildSignal inputs, outputs,
     * ordering, thresholds, persistence, or any Production execution path.
     */
    public void beginReplayAnalysisProfiling(long runId) {
        replayAnalysisProfiler.set(new ReplayAnalysisProfiler(runId));
    }

        replayAnalysisProfiler.remove();
        if (profiler == null) {

                normaliseInterval(intervalCode);

                new ArrayList<>(
                        maxOpenTime == null

                                        normalisedSymbol,
                                        normalisedInterval,
                                        PageRequest.of(0, HISTORY_LIMIT)
                                )
                                : candleRepository.findClosedCandlesAtOrBefore(
                                        normalisedSymbol,
                                        normalisedInterval,
                                        maxOpenTime,
                                        PageRequest.of(0, HISTORY_LIMIT)
                                )
                );

        if (candles.size() < MINIMUM_CANDLES) {
            log.warn(
                    "Indicator calculation skipped: symbol={}, interval={}, closedCandles={}, required={}",
                    normalisedSymbol,
                    normalisedInterval,
                    candles.size(),
                    MINIMUM_CANDLES
            );
            return Optional.empty();
        }

package com.crypto.service;

import com.crypto.domain.SignalDecision;
import com.crypto.domain.TradeSignal;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;

/**
 * Pure FIX-154 BUY-context assessment. It deliberately returns effective
 * decisions separately and never mutates the JPA-managed context signals.
 */
@Service
public class HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService {
    public static final int HARD_CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD = 70;

    public Assessment assess(TradeSignal fiveMinute, boolean fiveFresh,
                             TradeSignal oneHour, boolean oneFresh) {
        if (!fiveFresh) return Assessment.reject("MISSING_5M_CONFIRMATION", Band.NOT_APPLICABLE, fiveMinute, oneHour);
        if (!oneFresh) return Assessment.reject("MISSING_1H_CONTEXT", Band.NOT_APPLICABLE, fiveMinute, oneHour);

        SignalDecision five = decision(fiveMinute);
        SignalDecision one = decision(oneHour);
        if (bearish(five)) {
            boolean hard = hard(oneHour);
            return Assessment.reject("5M_BEARISH_VETO", hard ? Band.HARD_BEARISH_CANDIDATE : band(oneHour), fiveMinute, oneHour)
                    .withHardConfirmation(hard);
        }
        if (bearish(one) && !qualityValid(oneHour)) {
            return Assessment.reject("1H_BEARISH_VETO", Band.NOT_TRUSTED, fiveMinute, oneHour);
        }
        if (hard(oneHour)) {
            return Assessment.reject("HTF_TRUSTED_BEARISH_VETO", Band.HARD_BEARISH_CANDIDATE, fiveMinute, oneHour);
        }
        if (soft(oneHour)) {
            return Assessment.allow(Band.SOFT_CONFLICT, five, SignalDecision.NEUTRAL, 25, fiveMinute, oneHour);
        }
        return Assessment.allow(qualityValid(oneHour) ? Band.NO_CONFLICT : Band.NOT_TRUSTED,
                five, one, null, fiveMinute, oneHour);
    }

    private Band band(TradeSignal one) {
        if (!qualityValid(one)) return Band.NOT_TRUSTED;
        if (hard(one)) return Band.HARD_BEARISH_CANDIDATE;
        if (soft(one)) return Band.SOFT_CONFLICT;
        return Band.NO_CONFLICT;
    }
    private boolean hard(TradeSignal s) { return qualityValid(s) && decision(s) == SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL && confidence(s) >= HARD_CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD; }
    private boolean soft(TradeSignal s) { return qualityValid(s) && (decision(s) == SignalDecision.SELL || (decision(s) == SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL && confidence(s) < HARD_CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD)); }
    private boolean qualityValid(TradeSignal s) { return s != null && Boolean.TRUE.equals(s.getDataQualityValid()); }
    private int confidence(TradeSignal s) { return s == null ? 0 : s.getConfidenceScore(); }
    private SignalDecision decision(TradeSignal s) { return s == null || s.getDecision() == null ? SignalDecision.NEUTRAL : s.getDecision(); }
    private boolean bearish(SignalDecision d) { return d == SignalDecision.SELL || d == SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL; }

    public enum Band { NOT_APPLICABLE, NOT_TRUSTED, SOFT_CONFLICT, HARD_BEARISH_CANDIDATE, NO_CONFLICT }
    public record Assessment(boolean allowed, String code, Band band, SignalDecision effectiveFiveMinute,
                             SignalDecision effectiveOneHour, Integer maxInitialPercent,
                             boolean hardConfirmationPresent, TradeSignal rawFiveMinute, TradeSignal rawOneHour) {
        static Assessment reject(String code, Band band, TradeSignal five, TradeSignal one) {
            return new Assessment(false, code, band, decisionOf(five), decisionOf(one), null, false, five, one);
        }
        static Assessment allow(Band band, SignalDecision five, SignalDecision one, Integer cap, TradeSignal rawFive, TradeSignal rawOne) {
            return new Assessment(true, "HTF_CONTEXT_ALLOWED", band, five, one, cap, false, rawFive, rawOne);
        }
        Assessment withHardConfirmation(boolean value) { return new Assessment(allowed, code, band, effectiveFiveMinute, effectiveOneHour, maxInitialPercent, value, rawFiveMinute, rawOneHour); }
        private static SignalDecision decisionOf(TradeSignal s) { return s == null || s.getDecision() == null ? SignalDecision.NEUTRAL : s.getDecision(); }
    }
}
            MarketRegimeAssessment regime,
            MarketContextSnapshot context,
            MultiTimeframeConfluenceResult confluence,
            BtcMarketContextResult btcContext,
            DerivativesPositioningResult derivatives,
            OrderBookLiquidityResult liquidity
    ) {
        List<DecisionAdjustment> path = new ArrayList<>();
        SignalDecision current = baseDecision;
        boolean entryAllowed = strategy.entryAllowed();
        int sequence = 1;

        path.add(new DecisionAdjustment(
                sequence++, "STRATEGY_SCORE", DecisionAdjustmentType.BASE,
                baseDecision, baseDecision, true, entryAllowed,
                "The selected " + strategy.strategy() + " profile produced the isolated decision."
        ));

        if (!context.dataValid()) {
            SignalDecision before = current;
            current = safeNeutral(current);
            entryAllowed = false;
            path.add(new DecisionAdjustment(
                    sequence++, "DATA_QUALITY", DecisionAdjustmentType.VETO,
                    before, current, true, false,
                    "Required market data was incomplete or invalid; new entry was blocked."
            ));
        } else {
            path.add(new DecisionAdjustment(
                    sequence++, "DATA_QUALITY", DecisionAdjustmentType.PASS,
                    current, current, entryAllowed, entryAllowed,
                    "Required technical and contextual market data passed validation."
            ));
        }

        if (atrAdjustedDecision != baseDecision) {
            SignalDecision before = current;
            current = atrAdjustedDecision;
            entryAllowed = false;
            path.add(new DecisionAdjustment(
                    sequence++, "ATR_RISK", DecisionAdjustmentType.VETO,
                    before, current, true, false,
                    atrRisk.explanation()
            ));
        } else if (!atrRisk.immediateEntryAllowed() && isBullish(current)) {
            // Preserve the BUY recommendation, but do not execute at the current price.
            // The dashboard exposes PULLBACK_ENTRY or WAIT_FOR_RETRACEMENT.
            boolean entryBefore = entryAllowed;
            entryAllowed = false;
            path.add(new DecisionAdjustment(
                    sequence++, "ATR_RISK", DecisionAdjustmentType.DOWNGRADE,
                    current, current, entryBefore, false,
                    atrRisk.explanation()
            ));
        } else {
            path.add(new DecisionAdjustment(
                    sequence++, "ATR_RISK", DecisionAdjustmentType.PASS,
                    current, current, entryAllowed, entryAllowed,
                    atrRisk.explanation()
            ));
        }

        // FIX-042: activate the previously orphaned FIX-036 RANGE entry-location guard.
        // This is deliberately a one-way veto only: it never upgrades/downgrades the technical
        // decision, never changes strategy scores, and is not applied to non-range strategies.
        // Keeping it in FinalDecisionService makes the veto visible in the immutable decision path
        // and guarantees Production/Replay parity through the shared AnalysisService pipeline.
        if (rangeEntryLocation != null && rangeEntryLocation.applicable()) {
            boolean before = entryAllowed;
            boolean after = entryAllowed && rangeEntryLocation.entryAllowed();
            path.add(new DecisionAdjustment(
                    sequence++, "RANGE_ENTRY_LOCATION",
                    before && !after ? DecisionAdjustmentType.VETO : DecisionAdjustmentType.PASS,
                    current, current, before, after, rangeEntryLocation.explanation()
            ));
            entryAllowed = after;
        } else {
            path.add(new DecisionAdjustment(
                    sequence++, "RANGE_ENTRY_LOCATION", DecisionAdjustmentType.PASS,
                    current, current, entryAllowed, entryAllowed,
                    rangeEntryLocation == null
                            ? "Range entry-location guard was unavailable; no additional veto was applied."
                            : rangeEntryLocation.explanation()
            ));
        }

        current = appendExternalAdjustment(path, sequence++, "MULTI_TIMEFRAME", current,
                confluence.finalDecision(), entryAllowed,
                entryAllowed && confluence.entryAllowed(), confluence.explanation(),
                confluence.status() == ConfluenceStatus.UNAVAILABLE);
        entryAllowed = entryAllowed && confluence.entryAllowed();

        current = appendExternalAdjustment(path, sequence++, "BTC_CONTEXT", current,
                btcContext.finalDecision(), entryAllowed,
                entryAllowed && btcContext.entryAllowed(), btcContext.explanation(),
                btcContext.contextStatus() == BtcContextStatus.UNAVAILABLE
                        || btcContext.contextStatus() == BtcContextStatus.LEARNING);
        entryAllowed = entryAllowed && btcContext.entryAllowed();

        current = appendExternalAdjustment(path, sequence++, "DERIVATIVES_POSITIONING", current,
                derivatives.finalDecision(), entryAllowed,
                entryAllowed && derivatives.entryAllowed(), derivatives.explanation(),
                derivatives.status() == DerivativesPositioningStatus.UNAVAILABLE
                        || derivatives.status() == DerivativesPositioningStatus.LEARNING
                        || derivatives.status() == DerivativesPositioningStatus.NOT_APPLICABLE);
        entryAllowed = entryAllowed && derivatives.entryAllowed();

        current = appendExternalAdjustment(path, sequence, "ORDER_BOOK", current,
                liquidity.finalDecision(), entryAllowed,
                entryAllowed && liquidity.entryAllowed(), liquidity.explanation(),
                liquidity.status() == LiquidityContextStatus.UNAVAILABLE
                        || liquidity.status() == LiquidityContextStatus.LEARNING);
        entryAllowed = entryAllowed && liquidity.entryAllowed();

        if (!strategy.entryAllowed() && isBullish(current)) {
            current = SignalDecision.WATCH;
            entryAllowed = false;
        }

        // FIX-091 / Fix 3: compute confidence once without the legacy 49 cap, then derive
        // the effective value from final entry authority. This keeps 49 as a consequence
        // of a veto instead of hiding the actual signal quality that existed before it.
        int rawConfidence = rawConfidence(regime, context, confluence, btcContext, derivatives, liquidity);
        int effectiveConfidence = entryAllowed ? rawConfidence : Math.min(rawConfidence, 49);
        String primaryBlockingStage = path.stream()
                .filter(a -> a.entryAllowedBefore() && !a.entryAllowedAfter())
                .map(DecisionAdjustment::source)
                .findFirst()
                .orElse(entryAllowed ? null : "STRATEGY_AUTHORITY");
        String explanation = "FinalDecisionService applied " + path.size()
                + " ordered checks. Raw confidence " + rawConfidence + "/100, effective confidence "
                + effectiveConfidence + "/100. Entry " + (entryAllowed ? "allowed" : "blocked")
                + (primaryBlockingStage == null ? "." : " by " + primaryBlockingStage + ".");

        return new FinalDecisionResult(
                baseDecision,
                current,
                entryAllowed,
                rawConfidence,
                effectiveConfidence,
                primaryBlockingStage,
                List.copyOf(path),
                explanation
        );
    }

The automatic resolver is valid only for the paper-trading phase, where every
protection effect commits in the same transaction as its `APPLIED` update.
Before deployment, the code must prove that there are no asynchronous,
`REQUIRES_NEW`, in-memory-only, or external effects in this boundary. A future
live-exchange order needs a separate idempotent/exchange-reconciliation design.

The target runtime also requires exactly one active trader process. This is an
operational prerequisite, not a database lease or fence.

## Pending: FIX-154 higher-timeframe BUY validation

FIX-154 is separate from the local-candle change and is not implemented.

Its purpose is to let a historical bearish 1h signal affect a current 1m BUY
only when the historical evidence is fresh, quality-valid, and sufficiently
strong. `btcContextEntryAllowed=false` on a historical signal is investigatory
evidence, not a higher-timeframe quality gate; the current 1m BTC protection
remains unchanged.

The design includes reliability bands (`NOT_TRUSTED`, `SOFT_CONFLICT`,
`HARD_BEARISH_CANDIDATE`), required fresh/quality-valid bearish 5m support for
a hard veto, persisted `trade_signal.data_quality_valid`, recovery release
rules, immutable outcome audit evidence, and Replay/no-look-ahead parity.

The implementation design is maintained in `codex-docs/FIX-154_TECHNICAL_DESIGN.md`.
It supersedes no existing deployed behavior and is deliberately separate from
the unrelated legacy readiness document under `src/main/resources/md/FIX-146.md`.

## Deployment posture

Neither FIX-153 nor FIX-154 is deployment-ready. FIX-153 source hardening is
implemented but unverified; FIX-154 remains pending.

Required validation includes Java 21 build/tests, disposable MySQL bootstrap,
real-MySQL concurrency and lock-wait tests, crash/restart tests, stale-input
tests, Replay parity checks, OFF-mode health/admin checks, collector-free
runtime verification, deterministic dispatcher/detector/drain tests,
two-process Windows lock rehearsal, and sustained-load observation across
enabled symbols and intervals. This local workspace has Java 17 and cannot
compile the Java 21 project.

No production configuration, database, service, or deployment change is made
without explicit user approval.

## FIX-153 validation status (2026-10-09)

The reconciliation component now has deterministic Java 21 coverage for its
core admission and outcome contract. The tests execute the real audit and
outcome stores against H2 while mocking only Binance and the candle write
boundary. They prove that healthy delayed live closes are not taken over and
that recovery remains token-free/non-live when it is positively evidenced.

The real-MySQL suite contains matching update-only and insert-only assertions,
but it requires a deliberately disposable loopback MySQL instance and is not
run by the ordinary unit-test goal. This is a release gate, not a warning that
can be waived. The shared Binance 429/418 admission gate, universal mutation
barrier, structural historical-only isolation, financial-effect reconciler,
atomicity/crash proofs, replacement-time position protection, Replay parity,
and soak validation remain incomplete. Therefore `local-candle.reconciliation.enabled`
must stay `false`.

The single `local-candle` configuration mapping is a startup invariant. It now
contains both reconciliation and existing local-runtime properties; a second
mapping is invalid YAML and prevents the Spring application context from
starting.

### Shared Binance REST admission

`BinanceRestAdmissionGate` owns one process-wide REST cooldown. It is consulted
immediately before every call through `BinanceMarketDataClient`; a 429/418
response extends the cooldown using `Retry-After` when it is supplied, otherwise
the bounded fallback. This protects all local REST consumers from repeatedly
calling Binance during a rate-limit/ban response. It is deliberately not part
of the WebSocket or protection path and is not a persisted trading fence.

### Outcome-schema startup invariant

The active signal-processing runtime writes `candle_analysis_outcome` for
terminal analysis evidence. V108 therefore ships with the FIX-154 release even
though local-candle reconciliation remains disabled. `CandleAnalysisOutcomeSchemaVerifier`
runs after Flyway and fails application startup with a clear message if the
table is absent; this prevents later processing transactions from failing only
when they attempt their first outcome write.
# FIX-154 implementation update Г?" 2026-10-10

`HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService` is the single pure assessment boundary for
the 5m/1h BUY context. It returns effective decisions separately from the JPA
entities, so a SOFT 1h conflict can be assessed as effective `NEUTRAL` without
ever changing the persisted signal. `TradeExecutionValidationService` uses it
for direct BUY and accumulated-evidence authority; `OpportunityConsolidationService`
uses the same assessment for consolidation. A trusted SOFT bearish 1h limits an
initial entry to 25%; a trusted HARD or unknown bearish 1h rejects it, while
the legacy 5m bearish veto keeps priority.

`buy_htf_assessment_audit` is append-only operational evidence. Its writer is
best-effort and uses a bounded dedicated executor: auditing never borrows an
execution transaction's database connection, is skipped in Replay, and cannot
be part of a BUY decision. The progressive add route retains its
existing raw-1h guard; it writes `SOFT_HTF_ADD_BLOCKED` evidence only after
that guard has already rejected an add.

`GET /api/fix154/health` exposes non-gating counters for untrusted bearish
blocks, soft caps, soft add blocks, and best-effort audit failures.

## FIX-153 automatic local-candle reconciliation Г?" enabled 2026-10-10

In `shared-market.mode=OFF`, `LocalCandleReconciliationService` runs every
five seconds on its dedicated single-worker executor. It finds eligible
missing or interrupted candle identities inside the data-quality window, reads
the authoritative Binance candle, and writes only verified final evidence.
An existing open row is finalized by an update-only path; a missing row uses a
plain insert-if-absent backfill path. Neither route creates a close token,
dispatches analysis, or executes a trade. The next genuine live close resumes
ordinary analysis after the quality window is complete.
# Local candle coverage check Г?" 2026-10-09

- Scope: read-only check of all 21 enabled symbols in `crypto_ai.candle`, over the two-hour window ending at the database UTC clock.
- Current state: all 63 enabled symbol/interval lanes (1m, 5m, 1h) have closed-candle records and current live ingestion. The newest 1m candle was 54 seconds old at the check.
- Continuity: 5m and 1h had no internal gaps in the inspected window. Twelve symbols had one missing 1m candle: the interval from `2026-10-08T22:18:00Z` to `2026-10-08T22:19:00Z` (KSA `01:18` to `01:19`). This coincides with the local application startup period; the check establishes the gap but does not prove its cause.
- Impact: current live signals are being generated, but the missing historical 1m candles should be reconciled before relying on uninterrupted historical indicator lineage for that startup window. No database data or code was changed by this check.

# Signal-staleness root cause Г?" 2026-10-09

- System Health CRITICAL signal-staleness alerts were confirmed as accurate for 1m and 5m analysis, not a dashboard timezone issue. At the check, candles continued through `2026-10-09T06:43:00Z` (1m) and `06:35:00Z` (5m), while the newest live signals were `04:34:03Z` (1m) and `04:30:07Z` (5m).
- The most recent 210-candle data-quality window has a synchronized gap for every enabled symbol: missing 1m open `2026-10-09T04:34:00Z` (KSA 07:34) and missing 5m open `2026-10-09T04:30:00Z` (KSA 07:30).
- Cause confirmed from the WebSocket lifecycle log: generation 1 was revoked at `2026-10-09T04:35:00.133951100Z`; generation 2 was admitted at `04:35:01.369103400Z` (1,236 ms interruption). The affected 1m and 5m candles had both closed at the reconnect boundary. Binance did not replay their final closed-kline events, leaving the already-created local rows open rather than finalized.
- `CandleDataQualityService` is configured with `minimum-candles: 210` and `maximum-missing-candles: 0`. Therefore one missing candle makes the entire relevant analysis window invalid. Runtime logs confirm `DATA_QUALITY_BLOCKED` with `Missing 1 candle(s) in the analysis window` for current 1m and 5m worker attempts.
- 1h has no comparable gap and produced a live signal at `2026-10-09T06:00:03Z` (KSA 09:00).
- No repair or configuration change was made. Correct remediation must restore authoritative missing candles rather than relaxing the zero-missing data-quality safety rule.

## Bounded local repair completed

- Authorized local repair finalized the 42 interrupted `RUNTIME` rows using Binance REST candles for the exact identities above: 21 x 1m and 21 x 5m.
- Each response was validated for the expected open and close timestamp before one transaction finalized only a row where `closed=0`. Existing closed candles, provenance, and close-transition tokens were not changed; no close-dispatch, analysis, signal, or trade was initiated by the repair.
- Verification: all 1m/5m 210-candle quality windows had zero gaps after the repair. The next live close produced new LIVE signals at `2026-10-09T07:30:07Z` for both 1m and 5m.

# ETHUSDT paper-trade 59 investigation Г?" 2026-10-09

- Scope: local `crypto_ai` paper trade `wallet_trade.id=59` for `ETHUSDT`; all database reads were bounded and read-only. Stored UTC timestamps are shown below with their KSA (`UTC+03:00`) interpretation.
- Entry: signal `trade_signal.id=69226` was generated at `2026-10-09T07:33:00.233691Z` (10:33:00.234 KSA) from the 1m candle opened at 10:32 KSA. It was a `BUY`, score 79, confidence 70, entry allowed, at 2502.77. The trade executed at `07:33:01.654548Z` (10:33:01.655 KSA), 1.42 seconds after the signal; this is not an execution-latency issue.
- Exit: `wallet_trade.id=60` closed the position at 2504.30 at `07:34:34.196100Z` (10:34:34.196 KSA) with `PROFIT_LOCK_EXIT`. The managed position recorded a high of 2505.80 and an activated profit-lock price of 2504.429502243252. The realized paper gain was about 0.02445 USDT before fees (0.015982291620 ETH x 1.53 USDT).
- Market path: ETH rose from 2485.88 at 07:54 KSA to 2502.77 at entry (+16.89, +0.68%). It reached 2505.56 at 10:25 KSA before the entry; entry was 2.79 (0.11%) below that high. The post-entry high was 2505.93 at 10:34 KSA, only 3.16 (0.13%) above entry, then the candle retraced. This supports the user's observation that entry occurred late in the observed move.
- Primary cause: the local candle-quality interruption stopped 1m ETH signals after 07:34 KSA. There were no ETH 1m signals from 07:34 to 10:26 KSA, the period containing most of the rise. The interrupted 1m and 5m rows were repaired at about 10:28 KSA, so analysis resumed only near the later consolidation/breakout attempt. This is a confirmed data-availability gap, not proof that the strategy would have bought near 07:54 KSA.
- Immediate signal sequence after recovery: at 10:30 KSA the 5m signal was a BUY but blocked by `RANGE_ENTRY_LOCATION`; at 10:31 KSA the 1m raw BUY was vetoed by a bearish BTC context; at 10:33 KSA BTC context became neutral and the 1m trend-following BUY was allowed. The entry signal itself explicitly recorded mixed higher-timeframe context (5m BUY, 1h NEUTRAL) and a weakening but ultra-close ask wall before its target.
- Assessment: the trade followed the implemented rules once local analysis resumed, and the trade was entered/exited promptly. It was nevertheless a late entry relative to the full 07:54Г?"10:33 KSA advance because the system lacked signal coverage during the main move. The data gap is an operational defect already covered by the local-candle recovery deployment gate. Whether the unobserved earlier period would have produced an executable BUY is uncertain without a controlled Replay run; none was started because that would create replay state.
# SHIBUSDT signal #89469 / position #35 Г?" 2026-10-10

- Scope: read-only local `crypto_ai` investigation of wallet trades `106`/`107`,
  paper position `35`, managed position `64`, and signal `89469`.
- Timestamp correction (2026-10-10): the preceding interpretation was wrong
  because the read-only investigation connection used MySQL's `SYSTEM` timezone
  (KSA), while the application connection explicitly sets its session to UTC.
  For position `35`, MySQL's KSA session renders `opened_at` as
  `2026-10-09 23:45:06.682175`; converting that instant to UTC yields
  `2026-10-09 20:45:06.682175`. The Inspector's main trade card parses backend
  timestamps as UTC and renders them in the browser-local timezone. Therefore,
  with a KSA browser, its displayed 23:45 entry time is correct. Do not add
  three hours to the value shown by this KSA-session SQL connection.
- Signal confirmation: `trade_signal.id=89469` was generated at
  `2026-10-09 23:45:03.498678` KSA (`20:45:03.498678` UTC), from the 1m
  candle opened at 23:44 KSA. It is `WATCH`, score 60, confidence 74,
  `WEAK_UPTREND`, `TREND_FOLLOWING`, and `final_entry_allowed=1`.
- Entry: `0.000005410`; stop `0.000005382950`; TP `0.000005433730`; the
  25%-size setup-wakeup route entered from a 5m BUY with 1h NEUTRAL context.
- Exit: highest recorded price before close was `0.000005430`, or 84.2815% of
  the original TP distance. The dynamic lock activated at `0.000005421865` and
  live price `0.000005420` breached it. At the breach the current 1m and 5m
  were WATCH and 1h was NEUTRAL. The shared exit policy therefore returned
  `PROFIT_LOCK_EXIT`; it requires bullish 1h plus supportive 5m, or WATCH 1h
  plus supportive 5m, to hold a breached lock.
- TP extension: not evaluated. Extension begins only once price is at or above
  the original TP. The recorded high remained below TP at the time of exit.
- Subsequent action: later local candles reached `0.000005500` at about 00:40
  KSA, above the original TP. This is hindsight, not evidence available at the
  exit. Local SHIB 1m/5m coverage ends at about 00:56 KSA, so any claimed
  continuation through 12:15 KSA is not verifiable from local candle evidence.
- Assessment: behavior followed the existing protection rule, without a
  confirmed execution or latency defect. A future exit-policy change could
  explicitly allow a near-TP WATCH/WATCH/NEUTRAL continuation hold under
  measurable momentum/structure conditions and a tighter safety floor; it
  requires a separate design and Replay validation.

# SHIBUSDT #89469 near-TP continuation investigation Г?" 2026-10-10

- Status: investigation approved; implementation is explicitly not approved.

- Hypothesis to test: a breached profit lock close to target may sometimes

- Important counter-hypothesis: `1h=NEUTRAL` is not positive continuation
  evidence merely because 1m/5m are `WATCH`. Relaxing that guard on distance

- Required evidence before a policy change: use historical Replay/production

  1h/5m context, regime, trend structure, momentum, retracement depth and
  target-progress band. Evaluate TP hit rate, retained profit, adverse move,
  drawdown, and false-hold frequency. No post-exit price may inform entry or
# FIX-154 higher-timeframe BUY rule Г?" 2026-10-10

- `data_quality_valid=true` is required before a bearish 1h signal can be
  treated as trusted. `NULL` is unknown and fails closed for bearish evidence.
- A trusted `STRONG_SELL` at `confidence_score >= 70` blocks the BUY.
- A trusted `SELL`, or trusted `STRONG_SELL` below 70, is a SOFT conflict:
  profiles receive effective 1h `NEUTRAL`, and any allowed initial BUY is
  capped at 25%.
- A bearish 5m still returns `5M_BEARISH_VETO` before any 1h result.
- Existing progressive/add rules retain their raw 1h requirement; a SOFT 1h
  never authorizes an add.

# FIX-153 candle continuity rule Г?" 2026-10-10

- Missing price evidence never becomes invented OHLCV data and never bypasses
  the data-quality gate.
- Automatic reconciliation may restore only one exact, final Binance candle.
  Its recovered/backfilled row has no close token and cannot directly cause
  analysis, a signal, or a trade.
- A later normal live close is the only route that resumes ordinary signal
  generation after a repaired quality window.
  BUY validation change. It specifies data-quality persistence, time-bounded
  evidence selection, trust bands, hard-veto/recovery conditions, immutable
  audit evidence, preservation of legacy `5M_BEARISH_VETO`, and Replay parity.
- Clarified that the legacy `src/main/resources/md/FIX-146.md` readiness
  document is not the FIX-154 implementation source.
- Design only: no Java, migration, database, Production, or Replay behavior
  changed.

# 2026-10-09 Г?" FIX-154 trusted-bearish policy decision

- Recorded the approved policy: HARD trusted bearish 1h vetoes; unknown bearish
  1h remains blocked; only a trusted SOFT bearish 1h may permit an otherwise
  valid first entry, capped at 25%, with all add paths blocked while SOFT.
- Defined legacy 5m veto precedence, append-only/best-effort audit behavior,
  post-deployment unknown-quality behavior, health counters, and distinct BNB
  #150 historical/replay regression cases.
- Read-only 30-day local evidence supports the fixed confidence split:
  `STRONG_SELL` has 203 rows at >=70 and 192 below 70.
- Design only: no Java, migration, or runtime behavior changed.

  ordinary signal processing depends on `candle_analysis_outcome`; reconciliation

# 2026-10-10 Г?" FIX-154 trusted higher-timeframe BUY assessment (implementation)

- **Problem:** every bearish 1h row had equal veto authority, including
  historical rows with unknown data-quality provenance; there was no durable
  explanation of the effective 5m/1h BUY context.
- **Root cause:** BUY validation and consolidation read raw `TradeSignal`
  decisions directly, with no assessment of quality or bearish strength.
- **Solution:** added `data_quality_valid` persistence to `TradeSignal` and
  `AnalysisService`; introduced `HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService`; wired it
  into direct BUY, accumulated-evidence authority, consolidation and
  observational progressive-add evidence. The SOFT 1h result is capped at 25%
  and uses immutable effective decisions; HARD and unknown bearish evidence
  vetoes. `V107` creates `data_quality_valid` and append-only
  `buy_htf_assessment_audit`.
- **Files/classes:** `TradeSignal`, `AnalysisService`,
  `HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService`, `TradeExecutionValidationService`,
  `OpportunityConsolidationService`, `ExecutionIntelligenceService`,
  `BuyHtfAssessmentAuditStore`, `V107__fix_154_trusted_higher_timeframe_buy.sql`,
  and focused tests.
- **Database impact:** new nullable `trade_signal.data_quality_valid`; no
  backfill. Historical `NULL` stays unknown. New append-only audit table.
- **Production / Replay impact:** the same shared validation service is used
  for both. Audit failure is explicitly non-blocking. V108 ships because live
  runtime outcome writers require its schema; reconciliation remains disabled.
- **Validation status:** focused Maven tests could not run in this shell because
  the active JDK reports `release version 21 not supported`; execute with the
  project Java 21 toolchain before release.
