### Q1. Why did it exit on profit lock instead of take-profit? [PE]
- **TP needed three ticks.** TP was 0.00000543373: entry 0.00000541 plus 3 A- the 1m ATR of 0.00000000791. Prices move in steps of 0.00000001, so reaching TP required a price of 0.00000544.
- **The lock armed at two ticks.**
  - 0.00000543 was 84.2815% of the way to TP, past the 70% activation level.
  - That counts as one completed 10% step, so the lock was set at 50% of the TP distance: 0.000005421865, about 1.19 ticks above entry.
  - The settings are confirmed twice: the runtime log shows `activationPct=70.0000`, and trade 107's stored message shows activation 70%, initial lock 40%, trail 10%.
- **One tick down triggered it.** 0.00000542 was at or below the lock and above the hard floor of 0.000005412705, so the exit policy ran.
- **What the exit policy saw:**

  | Timeframe | Signal | Label | Age at the trigger |
  |---|---|---|---|
  | 1m | 89795 | WATCH | 41.8 s |
  | 5m | 89724 | WATCH | 3 m 38.8 s |
  | 1h | 88327 | NEUTRAL | 58 m 37.1 s |

  The labels are stored in trade 107's message. The signal IDs are my inference from the signal times in DBQ-010 [AI, high confidence].
- **Neither hold rule matched.**
  - A hold needs either a 1h BUY/STRONG_BUY with a supportive 5m, or a 1h WATCH with a supportive 5m and a 1m that is not bearish.
  - A NEUTRAL 1h fails both.
  - The 1h was not bearish, and the 1m and 5m were not both bearish, so the rule fell through to the default `PROFIT_LOCK_EXIT`.
- **No accepted price reached TP.** The corrected evidence chain is in objection S1.
- **Classification:** a valid exit that followed the implemented rules. The decision came 94 ms after the price was observed. It was not stale data, an execution delay, a configuration problem or a logic defect.

### Q2. Why was there no continuation into 10 October? [PE]
- In production, the target-extension check (`PositionContinuationPolicy`) is called from only one place: the live TP branch. Every other caller is a Replay class. This position never entered the TP branch.
- The position closed at 23:58:43 KSA, 39 min 16 s before the 00:38 KSA candle. Nothing was open to extend.
- "12:38" means 00:38 KSA [AI from PE]. The query log records the user's earlier times on a 12-hour clock ("11:44", "12:15"), and the investigation run began at 01:09 KSA on 10 October.
- *Outcome only:* the market first traded at TP in the 00:04 KSA candle, about 5Г?"6 minutes after the exit. Whether an extension would have passed then depends on 1m trend and momentum scores that aren't in the evidence. The exit could not have known any of this.

### Q3. Why didn't protection re-check the remaining upside before selling? [PE unless marked]
- **The lock-breach rule reads three labels and nothing else.** It does not look at distance to TP, momentum, the order book or the advisory analysis.
- **A hold was impossible for the whole trade [AI from PE].**
  - The deciding input was the 1h label.
  - It was NEUTRAL from 20:00:06Z, before entry, and the next 1h signal (21:00:12Z) came after the exit and was also NEUTRAL.
  - The entry signal 89469 had already recorded "1h=NEUTRAL".
  - So any lock breach during this hold had to sell.
- **Entry and hold use different 1h standards [AI from PE].**
  - The entry was allowed under the BALANCED_STRONG profile, which accepts a bullish 5m with a NEUTRAL 1h.
  - A hold after a lock breach requires the 1h to be WATCH or better.
  - About 20 s after the exit, the same profile refused a new entry for signal 89815 (5m WATCH, 1h NEUTRAL).
- **The price grid compressed the protection layers [AI from PE].**
  - TP was only 2.373 ticks above entry.
  - The lock armed at +2 ticks and fired at +1.
  - Near-TP protection arms at 90% of the TP distance. On this grid that is the same +3 tick price as TP, and the TP branch runs first, so Near-TP protection could never arm (stored state: INACTIVE).
- **The advisory analysis said HOLD 15 times during the trade** (exit scores 0Г?"7 out of 25). Live protection never reads it, and the advisory service can only execute a stop-loss.
- **The signal path would most likely have sold anyway [AI from PE].** Its profit-lock exit reads no labels. The next 1m signal, 89815 at 0.00000542, would most likely have closed the position at the same price about 20 s later.
- **The protection levels ignore fees [AI from PE + ER].**
  - Paper trades record a fee of 0.
  - At 0.1% per side, the +0.046211 USDT gross gain becomes about Г^'0.0038 USDT net.
  - The lock's "protected" level (+0.22%) and the +0.05% hard floor are close to or below a 0.2% round-trip fee.
- The later price rise is outcome only.

**[ER] External research**
- [Binance Developer Docs, "WebSocket Streams" (Spot)](https://developers.binance.com/docs/binance-spot-api-docs/web-socket-streams). No publication date shown; fetched this round.
  - Finding: the kline stream "push[es] updates to the current klines/candlestick every second", with an update speed of "1000ms for 1s, 2000ms for the other intervals". Each update carries both the close `c` and the high `h`.
  - Limitation: the page doesn't say whether a push is skipped when nothing changed. During the hold, SHIB updates arrived 2 s to 59 s apart, which suggests pushes only on change [AI].
  - Relevance: protection sees the close at most once every 2 s. A price spike between updates shows up only in `h`, which protection does not use.
- [Binance Fee Schedule](https://www.binance.com/en/fee/schedule) and [exchangeInfo SHIBUSDT](https://api.binance.com/api/v3/exchangeInfo?symbol=SHIBUSDT), carried over from round 1 and not re-fetched.
  - Findings: 0.100% regular spot fee per side; SHIB price step 0.00000001.
  - Limitations: the account's fee tier is unknown; exchangeInfo is a current snapshot, not the value at entry.

## OBJECTIONS
**To my own round 4**
- **S1. A step in my proof was wrong (the conclusion is not).**
  - I argued that because the profit-lock evaluator logged nothing between 20:47:41.566Z and 20:58:09.534Z, no accepted price went above 0.00000542.
  - That only rules out 0.00000543. A price at or above TP enters the TP branch and returns at `LivePositionProtectionService.java:170`, before the lock evaluator runs at `:185`, so it would never produce a lock log.
  - "No accepted price reached TP" rests instead on five points:
    - (a) The whole archive has zero lines matching `(TAKE_PROFIT|TP_EXTENSION).*SHIBUSDT`. Both possible TP outcomes, extending the target or selling, write log lines that include the symbol (`:120, :164, :168`).
    - (b) The TP stored on managed position 64 never changed.
    - (c) There is no TAKE_PROFIT trade; trade IDs 106 and 107 are consecutive.
    - (d) A silent failed sell is ruled out: position quantity is checked at `:93` in the same transaction, and SELL 107 sold the full quantity with no trade in between.
    - (e) The protection-failure and pending-resolved counters stayed at 0.
- **S2. One claim was right but poorly supported.** I wrote "protection only ever accepted 0.00000541Г+'0.00000542Г+'0.00000543Г+'0.00000542", but before the lock armed, the logs only record new highs, not every price. It is still true, now supported by:
  - the lowest 1m low from 20:45Z being 0.00000541 (DBQ-008);
  - the new-high logs, which never exceed 0.00000543;
  - point S1, which rules out anything at or above TP.
- **S3. "The three patterns cover every rejection path" was overstated.** They cover every rejection inside price-event handling, but not messages dropped earlier in the pipeline:
  - a WebSocket connection-gate rejection (`MESSAGE_REJECTED`, which doesn't name the symbol);
  - a failed callback (`CALLBACK_FAILED`);
  - a drain during shutdown (`KLINE_REJECTED_DRAINING`).

  Neither agent searched for these. Gate rejections are ruled out because the gate only ever moves forward (pending Г+' admitting Г+' revoked) and was still admitting SHIB messages at 20:58:00 and 20:58:43. Drain only happens at shutdown, and the counters show no restart. Failed-callback lines remain unsearched (Unknown 4).
- **S4. "105 ms to the SELL" needs qualifying.** That is the timestamp set inside the transaction. The exit was logged and committed at 20:58:43.837/.839Z, 390 ms after the price was observed. Still no material delay.

**To Codex (round 4 as supplied, round 5 on disk); all non-material**
- **C1.** "Below TP Г?▌ but below the lock" should say "and below the lock". 20:58:09.444Z is when the price was observed; the lock was saved at 20:58:09.534Z.
- **C2.** Codex's "silent failed sell" objection is ruled out by S1(d). The only residual is an unrecorded manual wallet edit, with no evidence of one.
- **C3.** The "if 12:38 meant noon" hedge is resolved (see Q2).
- **C4.** Codex's round-5 point, that the policy doesn't check whether the labels are fresh, is true of the code (`:95-97`). In this trade, though, all three labels were the newest available for their timeframes. Any breach late in an hour will see a 1h label close to an hour old. This describes the implemented rule, not a defect in this trade.
- **C5.** Codex missed the same two gaps I corrected in S1 and S3. Its conclusions are unaffected.

**Process**
- Query IDs DBQ-004, 005 and 006 were reused and their first results overwritten (`DATABASE_QUERY_LOG.md:62-117` vs `:271-326`).
- The result files display KSA times, although the log policy says raw values are UTC. For example, DBQ-014's 23:58:09.534 is the runtime log's 20:58:09.534Z.
- DBQ-008's columns `first_open_utc` and `last_open_utc` are mislabelled; they hold KSA times.
- No conclusion depends on any of this.

**Statistics and Replay**
- One trade whose price later rose proves nothing about a better rule. Picking it because of what happened afterwards is outcome-selection bias. Any rule change would need multi-symbol, out-of-sample testing that accounts for fees, slippage and price order within each candle.
- Exact-price Replay follows the live order and would read the same price events, but it skips the signal-path exits.
- Fallback Replay applies target extension and lock holds to signal prices, which production's signal path does not do.
- Replay was not run, so I make no parity claim.

**Entry route (outside Q1Г?"Q3; not blocking)**
- **E1.** The wake-up route bought 1.1 s after 89469's own evaluation said "Wait for fresh evidence to rebuild".
  - The route accepts opportunities still marked BUILDING and does not re-check that blocker.
  - Its message "after the prior blocker cleared" is fixed text (`ExecutionIntelligenceService.java:446`), not a condition the code verifies.
  - This is within the design, since the fresh 5m BUY counts as the new evidence, but the stored explanation overstates what was checked.
- **E2.** Risk/reward was 0.877: stop 0.5% below entry, TP 0.44% above. The wake-up route has no minimum risk/reward check; other entry routes require 1.0Г?"1.25. This is a design observation, not a rule violation.

## UNKNOWNS
1. **Did Binance trade at 0.00000544 or above between 20:45Z and 20:54Z?**
   - The 1m candle highs for those minutes aren't in the evidence.
   - One bounded read-only query of `candle` (SHIBUSDT, 1m, 20:45Г?"20:54Z, `high_price`) would settle it.
   - The answer can't change rule compliance. It could change the story from "TP was never reached" to "TP was touched between 2-second updates".
   - [AI] That is unlikely: it would need a jump of two or more ticks and a retreat within one update window, with no 0.00000543 update in between.
2. **Which code was actually running is unproven.**
   - The working tree has uncommitted changes to the live protection, price-event, kline-ingestion and wallet-execution classes.
   - Corroboration: trade 107's stored text matches `PositionExitPolicy.java:88-92` and `DynamicProfitLockService.java:179-182, 193-195` word for word.

4. **Some log patterns were not searched.** Nobody searched the archive for `CALLBACK_FAILED` or `MESSAGE_REJECTED` lines.
5. **One candle is missing from the post-exit price series.**
   - DBQ-008 counts 71 closed 1m candles where 72 are expected.
   - The gap falls between 21:11Z and 21:56Z, after this exit, so it does not affect this trade.
6. **88327's exact WATCH threshold is unproven.**
   - Its total score was 57.
   - The base scoring bands put WATCH at 60 or above, but strategy profiles can use other thresholds.
7. **Fees and slippage for a live account are unknown.** The account's fee tier is unknown, and paper trades record a fee of 0.

## VERDICT
The gap analysis of this trade against the implemented rules is complete, with file, ID and log evidence.
- Q1: the profit lock fired because no accepted price reached TP, and the NEUTRAL 1h left neither hold rule available.
- Q2: target extension is evaluated only in the live TP branch, which was never entered.
- Q3: the lock-breach rule reads labels only, and the deciding 1h label was NEUTRAL for the whole hold.

My corrections fix the reasoning and precision of earlier claims, not the conclusions. The remaining unknowns are stated and cannot change rule compliance. No defect was found in this trade. The design observations are inputs for a future specification, not recommended changes:
- price-step geometry for low-priced coins;
- different hold rules in the live path, the signal path and fallback Replay;
- protection levels that ignore fees;
- the wake-up route's explanation text.

This is not a production-safety statement and does not authorise code changes.

    # Automatic, token-free repair of verified local-candle gaps. Recovery runs
    # only in OFF mode, never dispatches recovered candles, and never weakens
    # the data-quality gate with invented price data.
    enabled: true
    initial-delay-ms: 15000
    scan-delay-ms: 5000
    executor-size: 1
    request-budget-per-pass: 10
    # Provisional defaults; release requires measured live-final-event grace.
    live-close-grace-seconds: 10
    safety-margin-seconds: 2
server:
  port: ${SERVER_PORT:8080}
  servlet:
    session:
            if(remaining[0]>0) for(Candidate candidate:gapCandidates(exchangeNow,databaseNow)) {
                if(drain!=null&&drain.draining())return;
                if(remaining[0]--<=0)break;
                recover(candidate,exchangeNow,databaseNow,true);
            }
            log.debug("[FIX-153][RECONCILIATION_PASS] reason={}, exchangeNow={}, budget={}",reason,exchangeNow,requestBudget-remaining[0]);
        } catch(RuntimeException failure) {
            log.warn("[FIX-153][RECONCILIATION_PASS_FAILED] reason={}; no candle was treated as recovered",reason,failure);
        } finally {if(drain!=null)drain.completeReconciliation();}
    }

    private List<Candidate> openCandidates(Instant exchangeNow,Instant databaseNow){
        List<Candidate> result=new ArrayList<>();
        for(String interval:properties.getIntervals()) {
            long seconds=intervalSeconds(interval), window=Math.max(1,minimumCandles)*seconds;
            Instant lower=exchangeNow.minusSeconds(window);
            Instant eligible=exchangeNow.minusSeconds(liveCloseGraceSeconds+safetyMarginSeconds);
            Instant latestOpen=eligible.minusSeconds(seconds);
            result.addAll(jdbc.query("""
                SELECT symbol,interval_code,open_time FROM candle
                WHERE closed=0 AND interval_code=? AND open_time>=? AND open_time<=?
                  AND (ingestion_source IS NULL OR ingestion_source<>'BACKFILL')
                ORDER BY open_time ASC LIMIT 50
                """,(rs,n)->{
                    Instant open=rs.getTimestamp(3).toInstant();
                    return new Candidate(rs.getString(1),rs.getString(2),open,expectedClose(open,rs.getString(2)));
                },interval,Timestamp.from(lower),Timestamp.from(latestOpen)));
        }
        return result.stream().filter(c->audit.due(c.symbol,c.interval,c.openTime,databaseNow))
            .filter(this::positiveLossEvidence).toList();
    }

    /** Read-only discovery of absent expected identities in the active quality window. */
    private List<Candidate> gapCandidates(Instant exchangeNow,Instant databaseNow){
        List<Candidate> result=new ArrayList<>();
        for(String symbol:coins.enabledSymbols()) for(String interval:properties.getIntervals()) {
            long seconds=intervalSeconds(interval), window=Math.max(1,minimumCandles)*seconds;
            Instant eligible=exchangeNow.minusSeconds(liveCloseGraceSeconds+safetyMarginSeconds);
            Instant first=Instant.ofEpochSecond((exchangeNow.minusSeconds(window).getEpochSecond()/seconds)*seconds);
            Set<Instant> present=new HashSet<>(jdbc.query("""
                SELECT open_time FROM candle WHERE symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND open_time>=? AND open_time<?
                """,(rs,n)->rs.getTimestamp(1).toInstant(),symbol,interval,Timestamp.from(first),Timestamp.from(eligible)));
            for(Instant open=first;open.plusSeconds(seconds).compareTo(eligible)<=0;open=open.plusSeconds(seconds)) {
                if(!present.contains(open) && audit.due(symbol,interval,open,databaseNow))
                    result.add(new Candidate(symbol,interval,open,open.plusSeconds(seconds).minusMillis(1)));
            }
        }
        return result;
    }

    private void recover(Candidate candidate,Instant exchangeNow,Instant databaseNow,boolean gap) {
        var prior=audit.findCandle(candidate.symbol,candidate.interval,candidate.openTime);
        int attempts=prior.map(s->s.attempts()+1).orElse(1);
        try {
            List<BinanceKline> rows=binance.getKlines(candidate.symbol,candidate.interval,candidate.openTime,candidate.closeTime);
            List<BinanceKline> exact=rows.stream().filter(k->k.openTime().equals(candidate.openTime)&&k.closeTime().equals(candidate.closeTime)).toList();
            if(rows.isEmpty() && gap) {
                List<BinanceKline> first=binance.getKlinesAtOrAfter(candidate.symbol,candidate.interval,candidate.openTime);
                if(!first.isEmpty() && first.getFirst().openTime().isAfter(candidate.openTime)) {
                    terminalUnavailable(candidate,exchangeNow,"NOT_ON_EXCHANGE");return;
                }
                pending(candidate,attempts,databaseNow,"REST_EMPTY",exchangeNow);return;
            }
            if(rows.isEmpty()) { pending(candidate,attempts,databaseNow,"REST_EMPTY",exchangeNow);return; }
            if(candidate.closeTime.compareTo(exchangeNow)>=0) {
                pending(candidate,attempts,databaseNow,"REST_NOT_FINAL",exchangeNow);return;
            }
            if(exact.size()!=1) {
                pending(candidate,attempts,databaseNow,"REST_MISMATCH",exchangeNow);return;
            }
            BinanceKline k=exact.getFirst();
            tx.executeWithoutResult(status->{
                boolean changed=gap ? candles.insertAuthoritativeGapBackfill(candidate.symbol,candidate.interval,candidate.openTime,k.closeTime(),
                    k.openPrice(),k.highPrice(),k.lowPrice(),k.closePrice(),k.volume(),k.quoteAssetVolume(),k.numberOfTrades(),k.takerBuyBaseVolume(),k.takerBuyQuoteVolume())
                    : candles.finalizeExistingOpenFromAuthoritative(candidate.symbol,candidate.interval,candidate.openTime,k.closeTime(),
                    k.openPrice(),k.highPrice(),k.lowPrice(),k.closePrice(),k.volume(),k.quoteAssetVolume(),k.numberOfTrades(),k.takerBuyBaseVolume(),k.takerBuyQuoteVolume());
                String result=changed ? (gap?"GAP_BACKFILLED":"RECOVERED") : (gap?"ALREADY_PRESENT":"ALREADY_CLOSED");
                audit.terminal(candidate.symbol,candidate.interval,candidate.openTime,result,null,exchangeNow,k.openTime(),k.closeTime());
                if(changed) outcomes.record(candidate.symbol,candidate.interval,candidate.openTime,"SKIPPED_NOT_LIVE",result,null,gap?"AUTHORITATIVE_GAP_BACKFILL":"AUTHORITATIVE_RECOVERY");
            });
        } catch(RuntimeException failure) { pending(candidate,attempts,databaseNow,"REST_FAILURE",exchangeNow); }
    }

    private void pending(Candidate c,int attempts,Instant databaseNow,String reason,Instant exchangeNow){
        long delay=Math.min(300,attempts==1?5:attempts==2?15:60L*(1L<<Math.min(2,attempts-3)));
        audit.pending(c.symbol,c.interval,c.openTime,attempts,databaseNow.plusSeconds(delay),reason,exchangeNow);
    }
    private boolean positiveLossEvidence(Candidate candidate){
        // This relies on the current FIX-131 invariant: one primary WebSocket
        // session invokes processKline synchronously and in Binance receive
        // order. If streams are sharded or processing becomes asynchronous,
        // this successor proof must be redesigned and re-tested.
        Instant successor=candidate.openTime.plusSeconds(intervalSeconds(candidate.interval));
        Integer successorPresent=jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM candle WHERE symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND open_time=?",Integer.class,
            candidate.symbol,candidate.interval,Timestamp.from(successor));
        return successorPresent!=null&&successorPresent>0 || (lastReconnectAdmission!=null && !lastReconnectAdmission.isBefore(candidate.closeTime));
    }
    private void terminalUnavailable(Candidate candidate,Instant exchangeNow,String reason){
        tx.executeWithoutResult(status->{
            audit.terminal(candidate.symbol,candidate.interval,candidate.openTime,"EXPIRED",reason,exchangeNow,null,null);
            outcomes.record(candidate.symbol,candidate.interval,candidate.openTime,"SKIPPED_DATA_UNAVAILABLE",reason,null,"AUTHORITATIVE_RECONCILIATION");
        });
    }
    private static long intervalSeconds(String interval){return switch(interval){case "1m"->60;case "5m"->300;case "15m"->900;case "1h"->3600;case "4h"->14400;case "1d"->86400;default->throw new IllegalArgumentException("Unsupported interval: "+interval);};}
    private static Instant expectedClose(Instant open,String interval){return open.plusSeconds(intervalSeconds(interval)).minusMillis(1);}
    private record Candidate(String symbol,String interval,Instant openTime,Instant closeTime) {}
}
package com.crypto.repository;

import com.crypto.domain.Candle;
import com.crypto.shared.SharedMarketSource;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Repository;
import org.springframework.jdbc.core.JdbcTemplate;
import org.springframework.jdbc.core.RowMapper;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Pageable;
import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.sql.Timestamp;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.*;

/** FIX-153: local candle facade in OFF; shared reads remain a rollback capability.
 * Exact predicates/order are retained. Candle is a DTO; SQL migrations own its schema.
 * Runtime and backfill write policies are deliberately distinct. */
@Repository
public class CandleRepository {
    private static final org.slf4j.Logger log=org.slf4j.LoggerFactory.getLogger(CandleRepository.class);
    private final SharedMarketSource source;
    private final JdbcTemplate local;
    public CandleRepository(SharedMarketSource source, JdbcTemplate local) { this.source=source; this.local=local; }
    public static final RowMapper<Candle> MAPPER = (r,n) -> Candle.builder()
        .id(r.getLong("id")).symbol(r.getString("symbol")).intervalCode(r.getString("interval_code"))
        .openTime(r.getTimestamp("open_time").toInstant()).closeTime(r.getTimestamp("close_time").toInstant())
        .openPrice(r.getBigDecimal("open_price")).highPrice(r.getBigDecimal("high_price"))
        .lowPrice(r.getBigDecimal("low_price")).closePrice(r.getBigDecimal("close_price"))
        .volume(r.getBigDecimal("volume")).quoteAssetVolume(r.getBigDecimal("quote_asset_volume"))
        .numberOfTrades(r.getObject("number_of_trades",Long.class))
        .takerBuyBaseVolume(r.getBigDecimal("taker_buy_base_volume"))
        .takerBuyQuoteVolume(r.getBigDecimal("taker_buy_quote_volume")).closed(r.getBoolean("closed")).build();
    private List<Candle> read(String tail,Object... args) {
        Object[] converted=Arrays.stream(args).map(v->v instanceof Instant i ? Timestamp.from(i) : v).toArray();
        List<Candle> rows=source.reader().query("SELECT * FROM candle WHERE "+tail,MAPPER,converted);
        com.crypto.shared.CandleInputAudit.capture(tail,rows);
        return rows;
    }
    private String page(Pageable p) { return p.isUnpaged()? "" : " LIMIT "+p.getPageSize()+" OFFSET "+p.getOffset(); }
    public Optional<Candle> findBySymbolAndIntervalCodeAndOpenTime(String s,String i,Instant t) {
        return read("symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND open_time=?",s,i,t).stream().findFirst();
    }
    public List<Candle> findTop500BySymbolAndIntervalCodeOrderByOpenTimeDesc(String s,String i) {
        return read("symbol=? AND interval_code=? ORDER BY open_time DESC LIMIT 500",s,i);
    }
    public List<Candle> findTop200BySymbolAndIntervalCodeAndClosedTrueOrderByOpenTimeDesc(String s,String i) {
        return read("symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND closed=1 ORDER BY open_time DESC LIMIT 200",s,i);
    }
    public List<Candle> findClosedCandles(String s,String i,Pageable p) {
        return read("symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND closed=1 ORDER BY open_time DESC"+page(p),s,i);
    }
    public List<Candle> findClosedCandlesAtOrBefore(String s,String i,Instant t,Pageable p) {
        return read("symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND closed=1 AND open_time<=? ORDER BY open_time DESC"+page(p),s,i,t);
    }
    public List<Candle> findClosedCandlesClosedAtOrBefore(String s,String i,Instant t,Pageable p) {
        return read("symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND closed=1 AND close_time<=? ORDER BY open_time DESC"+page(p),s,i,t);
    }
    public List<Candle> findBySymbolAndIntervalCodeAndClosedTrueAndOpenTimeBetweenOrderByOpenTimeAsc(String s,String i,Instant a,Instant b) {
        return read("symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND closed=1 AND open_time BETWEEN ? AND ? ORDER BY open_time ASC",s,i,a,b);
    }
    public List<Candle> findBySymbolAndIntervalCodeAndOpenTimeBetweenOrderByOpenTimeAsc(String s,String i,Instant a,Instant b) {
        return read("symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND open_time BETWEEN ? AND ? ORDER BY open_time ASC",s,i,a,b);
    }
    public List<Candle> findBySymbolAndIntervalCodeAndClosedTrueOrderByOpenTimeAsc(String s,String i) {
        return read("symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND closed=1 ORDER BY open_time ASC",s,i);
    }
    public Optional<Candle> findFirstBySymbolAndIntervalCodeAndClosedTrueOrderByCloseTimeDesc(String s,String i) {
        return read("symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND closed=1 ORDER BY close_time DESC LIMIT 1",s,i).stream().findFirst();
    }
    public Optional<Candle> findFirstBySymbolAndIntervalCodeAndClosedTrueOrderByOpenTimeAsc(String s,String i) {
        return read("symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND closed=1 ORDER BY open_time ASC LIMIT 1",s,i).stream().findFirst();
    }
    public List<String> findDistinctSymbols() { return source.reader().queryForList("SELECT DISTINCT symbol FROM candle ORDER BY symbol",String.class); }
    public long countBySymbolAndIntervalCodeAndClosedTrue(String s,String i) {
        return source.reader().queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM candle WHERE symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND closed=1",Long.class,s,i);
    }
    public List<Candle> findClosedCandlesMissingAnalysisThrough(String s,String i,Instant a,Instant b,Pageable p) {
        // Shared credentials never need access to Trader's indicators/signals. Fetch
        // local identities separately, then paginate AFTER applying the missing test.
        Set<Instant> indicators=new HashSet<>(local.query("SELECT candle_open_time FROM technical_indicator WHERE symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND candle_open_time BETWEEN ? AND ?",
            (r,n)->r.getTimestamp(1).toInstant(),s,i,Timestamp.from(a),Timestamp.from(b)));
        Set<Instant> signals=new HashSet<>(local.query("SELECT candle_open_time FROM trade_signal WHERE symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND candle_open_time BETWEEN ? AND ?",
            (r,n)->r.getTimestamp(1).toInstant(),s,i,Timestamp.from(a),Timestamp.from(b)));
        long started=System.nanoTime();
        var candles=findBySymbolAndIntervalCodeAndClosedTrueAndOpenTimeBetweenOrderByOpenTimeAsc(s,i,a,b);
        long fetchMs=(System.nanoTime()-started)/1_000_000;
        var result=candles.stream()
            .filter(c->!indicators.contains(c.getOpenTime()) || !signals.contains(c.getOpenTime()))
            .skip(p.isUnpaged()?0:p.getOffset()).limit(p.isUnpaged()?Long.MAX_VALUE:p.getPageSize()).toList();
        // Measurement only. Fetch includes pool acquisition, transfer and mapping;
        // it is not labelled database execution or commit time. Filtering unchanged.
        if(source.enabled()) {
            String detail="fetched="+candles.size()+", returned="+result.size()+", fetchIncludingPoolMs="+fetchMs+", pool="+source.poolPressure();
            log.info("[FIX-132][RECOVERY_SCAN] symbol={}, interval={}, from={}, through={}, {}",s,i,a,b,detail);
            try { local.update("INSERT INTO shared_market_health(component,status,detail) VALUES(?,'MEASURED',?) ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE status=VALUES(status),detail=VALUES(detail)","RECOVERY:"+s+":"+i,detail); }
            catch(RuntimeException unavailable) {log.warn("[FIX-132][RECOVERY_METRIC_NOT_PERSISTED] symbol={}, interval={}",s,i,unavailable);}
        }
        return result;
    }
    public int upsert(String symbol,String intervalCode,Instant openTime,Instant closeTime,
        BigDecimal openPrice,BigDecimal highPrice,BigDecimal lowPrice,BigDecimal closePrice,
        BigDecimal volume,BigDecimal quoteAssetVolume,Long numberOfTrades,BigDecimal takerBuyBaseVolume,
        BigDecimal takerBuyQuoteVolume,boolean closed) {
        source.requireLocalWriter();
        return write(symbol,intervalCode,openTime,closeTime,openPrice,highPrice,lowPrice,closePrice,
            volume,quoteAssetVolume,numberOfTrades,takerBuyBaseVolume,takerBuyQuoteVolume,closed,"BACKFILL",null);
    }
    /** FIX-153: result is valid only after the caller's INPUT transaction commits.
     * Token read-back compares immutable receipt identity, never a racy pre-read of closed state. */
    public Transition upsertRuntime(String symbol,String intervalCode,Instant openTime,Instant closeTime,
        BigDecimal openPrice,BigDecimal highPrice,BigDecimal lowPrice,BigDecimal closePrice,
        BigDecimal volume,BigDecimal quoteAssetVolume,Long numberOfTrades,BigDecimal takerBuyBaseVolume,
        BigDecimal takerBuyQuoteVolume,boolean closed,String token) {
        source.requireLocalWriter();
        write(symbol,intervalCode,openTime,closeTime,openPrice,highPrice,lowPrice,closePrice,
            volume,quoteAssetVolume,numberOfTrades,takerBuyBaseVolume,takerBuyQuoteVolume,closed,"RUNTIME",closed?token:null);
        return local.queryForObject("SELECT closed,close_time,close_transition_token,ingestion_source FROM candle WHERE symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND open_time=?",
            // Provenance deliberately does not participate here. A legacy NULL row that
            // the live stream closes is a genuine close transition; BACKFILL never writes
            // a token, so it cannot manufacture a dispatchable transition.
            (rs,n)->new Transition(closed && token.equals(rs.getString(3)),
                rs.getTimestamp(2).toInstant(),rs.getString(3),rs.getBoolean(1)),symbol,intervalCode,Timestamp.from(openTime));
    }
    /** `alreadyClosed` is diagnostic only; the matching token is the sole dispatch authority. */
    public record Transition(boolean transitionedToClosed,Instant closeTime,String token,boolean alreadyClosed) {}

    /**
     * Authoritative recovery is intentionally update-only.  Unlike ordinary
     * REST backfill it can never insert an absent identity and it never makes a
     * runtime close token.  This keeps a late recovery from stealing a normal
     * live close admission.
     */
    public boolean finalizeExistingOpenFromAuthoritative(String symbol,String intervalCode,Instant openTime,Instant closeTime,
        BigDecimal openPrice,BigDecimal highPrice,BigDecimal lowPrice,BigDecimal closePrice,
        BigDecimal volume,BigDecimal quoteAssetVolume,Long numberOfTrades,BigDecimal takerBuyBaseVolume,
        BigDecimal takerBuyQuoteVolume) {
        source.requireLocalWriter();
        return local.update("""
            UPDATE candle SET close_time=?,open_price=?,high_price=?,low_price=?,close_price=?,volume=?,
              quote_asset_volume=?,number_of_trades=?,taker_buy_base_volume=?,taker_buy_quote_volume=?,
              closed=1,updated_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6)
            WHERE symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND open_time=? AND closed=0
              AND (ingestion_source IS NULL OR ingestion_source<>'BACKFILL')
            """, Timestamp.from(closeTime),openPrice,highPrice,lowPrice,closePrice,volume,quoteAssetVolume,
            numberOfTrades,takerBuyBaseVolume,takerBuyQuoteVolume,symbol,intervalCode,Timestamp.from(openTime)) == 1;
    }

    /**
     * Exact authoritative gap repair is pure insert-if-absent.  Do not replace
     * this with ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE: a live row may arrive while REST is in
     * flight and must remain untouched so its normal close can own the token.
     */
    public boolean insertAuthoritativeGapBackfill(String symbol,String intervalCode,Instant openTime,Instant closeTime,
        BigDecimal openPrice,BigDecimal highPrice,BigDecimal lowPrice,BigDecimal closePrice,
        BigDecimal volume,BigDecimal quoteAssetVolume,Long numberOfTrades,BigDecimal takerBuyBaseVolume,
        BigDecimal takerBuyQuoteVolume) {
        source.requireLocalWriter();
        try {
            return local.update("""
                INSERT INTO candle(symbol,interval_code,open_time,close_time,open_price,high_price,low_price,close_price,
                    volume,quote_asset_volume,number_of_trades,taker_buy_base_volume,taker_buy_quote_volume,
                    closed,ingestion_source,close_transition_token,created_at,updated_at)
                VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,1,'BACKFILL',NULL,CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6))
                """,symbol,intervalCode,Timestamp.from(openTime),Timestamp.from(closeTime),openPrice,highPrice,lowPrice,
                closePrice,volume,quoteAssetVolume,numberOfTrades,takerBuyBaseVolume,takerBuyQuoteVolume) == 1;
        } catch (org.springframework.dao.DuplicateKeyException duplicate) {
            return false;
        }
    }
    private int write(String symbol,String intervalCode,Instant openTime,Instant closeTime,
        BigDecimal openPrice,BigDecimal highPrice,BigDecimal lowPrice,BigDecimal closePrice,
        BigDecimal volume,BigDecimal quoteAssetVolume,Long numberOfTrades,BigDecimal takerBuyBaseVolume,
        BigDecimal takerBuyQuoteVolume,boolean closed,String provenance,String token) {
        String duplicateClause = "BACKFILL".equals(provenance) ? """
            ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE
                -- REST/backfill is authoritative enough to finish an interrupted open
                -- candle, but never changes a completed candle, provenance or token.
                close_time=IF(closed=1,close_time,VALUES(close_time)),
                open_price=IF(closed=1,open_price,VALUES(open_price)),
                high_price=IF(closed=1,high_price,VALUES(high_price)),
                low_price=IF(closed=1,low_price,VALUES(low_price)),
                close_price=IF(closed=1,close_price,VALUES(close_price)),
                volume=IF(closed=1,volume,VALUES(volume)),
                quote_asset_volume=IF(closed=1,quote_asset_volume,VALUES(quote_asset_volume)),
                number_of_trades=IF(closed=1,number_of_trades,VALUES(number_of_trades)),
                taker_buy_base_volume=IF(closed=1,taker_buy_base_volume,VALUES(taker_buy_base_volume)),
                taker_buy_quote_volume=IF(closed=1,taker_buy_quote_volume,VALUES(taker_buy_quote_volume)),
                updated_at=IF(closed=1,updated_at,CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6)),
                closed=GREATEST(closed,VALUES(closed))
            """ : """
            ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE
                close_time=IF(closed=1,close_time,VALUES(close_time)),
                open_price=IF(closed=1,open_price,VALUES(open_price)),
                high_price=IF(closed=1,high_price,VALUES(high_price)),
                low_price=IF(closed=1,low_price,VALUES(low_price)),
                close_price=IF(closed=1,close_price,VALUES(close_price)),
                volume=IF(closed=1,volume,VALUES(volume)),
                quote_asset_volume=IF(closed=1,quote_asset_volume,VALUES(quote_asset_volume)),
                number_of_trades=IF(closed=1,number_of_trades,VALUES(number_of_trades)),
                taker_buy_base_volume=IF(closed=1,taker_buy_base_volume,VALUES(taker_buy_base_volume)),
                taker_buy_quote_volume=IF(closed=1,taker_buy_quote_volume,VALUES(taker_buy_quote_volume)),
                close_transition_token=IF(closed=0 AND VALUES(closed)=1,VALUES(close_transition_token),close_transition_token),
                updated_at=IF(closed=1,updated_at,CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6)),
                closed=GREATEST(closed,VALUES(closed))
            """;
        // FIX-153: backfill inserts missing identities only, so it cannot fabricate a runtime close.
        return local.update("""
            INSERT INTO candle(symbol,interval_code,open_time,close_time,open_price,high_price,low_price,close_price,
                volume,quote_asset_volume,number_of_trades,taker_buy_base_volume,taker_buy_quote_volume,
                closed,ingestion_source,close_transition_token,created_at,updated_at)
            VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6))
            """ + duplicateClause,symbol,intervalCode,Timestamp.from(openTime),Timestamp.from(closeTime),openPrice,highPrice,lowPrice,
            closePrice,volume,quoteAssetVolume,numberOfTrades,takerBuyBaseVolume,takerBuyQuoteVolume,closed,provenance,token);
    }
}
-- FIX-154: immutable quality evidence belongs to each generated signal.
-- NULL is intentional for signals generated before this migration.
ALTER TABLE trade_signal
    ADD COLUMN data_quality_valid BOOLEAN NULL;

-- Execution-time evidence is append-only: the same reference signal can be
-- assessed repeatedly through different or repeated authority paths.
CREATE TABLE buy_htf_assessment_audit (
    id BIGINT NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
    reference_signal_id BIGINT NOT NULL,
    evaluation_path VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL,
    evaluated_at TIMESTAMP(6) NOT NULL,
    replay_mode BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    one_hour_signal_id BIGINT NULL,
    one_hour_raw_decision VARCHAR(30) NULL,
    one_hour_effective_decision VARCHAR(30) NULL,
    one_hour_candle_close_time TIMESTAMP(6) NULL,
    one_hour_age_seconds BIGINT NULL,
    one_hour_data_quality_valid BOOLEAN NULL,
    one_hour_usability_reason VARCHAR(96) NULL,
    five_minute_signal_id BIGINT NULL,
    five_minute_raw_decision VARCHAR(30) NULL,
    five_minute_effective_decision VARCHAR(30) NULL,
    five_minute_candle_close_time TIMESTAMP(6) NULL,
    five_minute_age_seconds BIGINT NULL,
    five_minute_data_quality_valid BOOLEAN NULL,
    five_minute_usability_reason VARCHAR(96) NULL,
    confidence_threshold INT NOT NULL,
    assessment_band VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL,
    max_initial_percent INT NULL,
    legacy_veto_result VARCHAR(64) NULL,
    final_result VARCHAR(96) NOT NULL,
    final_reason VARCHAR(1000) NULL,
    created_at TIMESTAMP(6) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),
    KEY idx_fix154_audit_reference (reference_signal_id, evaluated_at),
    KEY idx_fix154_audit_path_time (evaluation_path, evaluated_at),
    KEY idx_fix154_audit_band_time (assessment_band, created_at)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci;
-- FIX-154 forward-only completion. V107 was already applied on the trader
-- database; never edit it. Replay needs the mapped signal fields and the
-- immutable audit needs its hard-confirmation marker.
ALTER TABLE trade_signal_test
    ADD COLUMN btc_context_candle_close_time TIMESTAMP(6) NULL,
    ADD COLUMN btc_context_age_seconds BIGINT NULL,
    ADD COLUMN btc_context_freshness_threshold_seconds BIGINT NULL,
    ADD COLUMN signal_mode VARCHAR(24) NULL,
    ADD COLUMN data_quality_valid BOOLEAN NULL;

                if(drain!=null&&drain.draining())return;
                if(remaining[0]--<=0)break;
                recover(candidate,exchangeNow,databaseNow,true);

        } catch(RuntimeException failure) {
            log.warn("[FIX-153][RECONCILIATION_PASS_FAILED] reason={}; no candle was treated as recovered",reason,failure);
        } finally {if(drain!=null)drain.completeReconciliation();}
    }

    private List<Candidate> openCandidates(Instant exchangeNow,Instant databaseNow){
        List<Candidate> result=new ArrayList<>();
        for(String interval:properties.getIntervals()) {
            long seconds=intervalSeconds(interval), window=Math.max(1,minimumCandles)*seconds;
            Instant lower=exchangeNow.minusSeconds(window);
            Instant eligible=exchangeNow.minusSeconds(liveCloseGraceSeconds+safetyMarginSeconds);
            Instant latestOpen=eligible.minusSeconds(seconds);
            result.addAll(jdbc.query("""
                SELECT symbol,interval_code,open_time FROM candle
                WHERE closed=0 AND interval_code=? AND open_time>=? AND open_time<=?
                  AND (ingestion_source IS NULL OR ingestion_source<>'BACKFILL')
                ORDER BY open_time ASC LIMIT 50
                """,(rs,n)->{
                    Instant open=rs.getTimestamp(3).toInstant();
                    return new Candidate(rs.getString(1),rs.getString(2),open,expectedClose(open,rs.getString(2)));
                },interval,Timestamp.from(lower),Timestamp.from(latestOpen)));
        }
        return result.stream().filter(c->audit.due(c.symbol,c.interval,c.openTime,databaseNow))
            .filter(this::positiveLossEvidence).toList();
    }

    /** Read-only discovery of absent expected identities in the active quality window. */
    private List<Candidate> gapCandidates(Instant exchangeNow,Instant databaseNow){
        List<Candidate> result=new ArrayList<>();
        for(String symbol:coins.enabledSymbols()) for(String interval:properties.getIntervals()) {
            long seconds=intervalSeconds(interval), window=Math.max(1,minimumCandles)*seconds;
            Instant eligible=exchangeNow.minusSeconds(liveCloseGraceSeconds+safetyMarginSeconds);
            Instant first=Instant.ofEpochSecond((exchangeNow.minusSeconds(window).getEpochSecond()/seconds)*seconds);
            Set<Instant> present=new HashSet<>(jdbc.query("""
                SELECT open_time FROM candle WHERE symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND open_time>=? AND open_time<?
                """,(rs,n)->rs.getTimestamp(1).toInstant(),symbol,interval,Timestamp.from(first),Timestamp.from(eligible)));
            for(Instant open=first;open.plusSeconds(seconds).compareTo(eligible)<=0;open=open.plusSeconds(seconds)) {
                if(!present.contains(open) && audit.due(symbol,interval,open,databaseNow))
                    result.add(new Candidate(symbol,interval,open,open.plusSeconds(seconds).minusMillis(1)));
            }
        }
        return result;
    }

    private void recover(Candidate candidate,Instant exchangeNow,Instant databaseNow,boolean gap) {
        var prior=audit.findCandle(candidate.symbol,candidate.interval,candidate.openTime);
        int attempts=prior.map(s->s.attempts()+1).orElse(1);
        try {
            List<BinanceKline> rows=binance.getKlines(candidate.symbol,candidate.interval,candidate.openTime,candidate.closeTime);
            List<BinanceKline> exact=rows.stream().filter(k->k.openTime().equals(candidate.openTime)&&k.closeTime().equals(candidate.closeTime)).toList();
            if(rows.isEmpty() && gap) {
                List<BinanceKline> first=binance.getKlinesAtOrAfter(candidate.symbol,candidate.interval,candidate.openTime);
                if(!first.isEmpty() && first.getFirst().openTime().isAfter(candidate.openTime)) {
                    terminalUnavailable(candidate,exchangeNow,"NOT_ON_EXCHANGE");return;
                }
                pending(candidate,attempts,databaseNow,"REST_EMPTY",exchangeNow);return;
            }
            if(rows.isEmpty()) { pending(candidate,attempts,databaseNow,"REST_EMPTY",exchangeNow);return; }
            if(candidate.closeTime.compareTo(exchangeNow)>=0) {
                pending(candidate,attempts,databaseNow,"REST_NOT_FINAL",exchangeNow);return;
            }
            if(exact.size()!=1) {
                pending(candidate,attempts,databaseNow,"REST_MISMATCH",exchangeNow);return;
            }
            BinanceKline k=exact.getFirst();
            tx.executeWithoutResult(status->{
                boolean changed=gap ? candles.insertAuthoritativeGapBackfill(candidate.symbol,candidate.interval,candidate.openTime,k.closeTime(),
                    k.openPrice(),k.highPrice(),k.lowPrice(),k.closePrice(),k.volume(),k.quoteAssetVolume(),k.numberOfTrades(),k.takerBuyBaseVolume(),k.takerBuyQuoteVolume())
                    : candles.finalizeExistingOpenFromAuthoritative(candidate.symbol,candidate.interval,candidate.openTime,k.closeTime(),
                    k.openPrice(),k.highPrice(),k.lowPrice(),k.closePrice(),k.volume(),k.quoteAssetVolume(),k.numberOfTrades(),k.takerBuyBaseVolume(),k.takerBuyQuoteVolume());
                String result=changed ? (gap?"GAP_BACKFILLED":"RECOVERED") : (gap?"ALREADY_PRESENT":"ALREADY_CLOSED");
                audit.terminal(candidate.symbol,candidate.interval,candidate.openTime,result,null,exchangeNow,k.openTime(),k.closeTime());
                if(changed) outcomes.record(candidate.symbol,candidate.interval,candidate.openTime,"SKIPPED_NOT_LIVE",result,null,gap?"AUTHORITATIVE_GAP_BACKFILL":"AUTHORITATIVE_RECOVERY");
            });
        } catch(RuntimeException failure) { pending(candidate,attempts,databaseNow,"REST_FAILURE",exchangeNow); }
    }

    private void pending(Candidate c,int attempts,Instant databaseNow,String reason,Instant exchangeNow){
        long delay=Math.min(300,attempts==1?5:attempts==2?15:60L*(1L<<Math.min(2,attempts-3)));
        audit.pending(c.symbol,c.interval,c.openTime,attempts,databaseNow.plusSeconds(delay),reason,exchangeNow);
    }
    private boolean positiveLossEvidence(Candidate candidate){
        // This relies on the current FIX-131 invariant: one primary WebSocket
        // session invokes processKline synchronously and in Binance receive
        // order. If streams are sharded or processing becomes asynchronous,
        // this successor proof must be redesigned and re-tested.
        Instant successor=candidate.openTime.plusSeconds(intervalSeconds(candidate.interval));
        Integer successorPresent=jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM candle WHERE symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND open_time=?",Integer.class,
            candidate.symbol,candidate.interval,Timestamp.from(successor));
        return successorPresent!=null&&successorPresent>0 || (lastReconnectAdmission!=null && !lastReconnectAdmission.isBefore(candidate.closeTime));
    }
    private void terminalUnavailable(Candidate candidate,Instant exchangeNow,String reason){
        tx.executeWithoutResult(status->{
            audit.terminal(candidate.symbol,candidate.interval,candidate.openTime,"EXPIRED",reason,exchangeNow,null,null);
            outcomes.record(candidate.symbol,candidate.interval,candidate.openTime,"SKIPPED_DATA_UNAVAILABLE",reason,null,"AUTHORITATIVE_RECONCILIATION");
        });
    }
    private static long intervalSeconds(String interval){return switch(interval){case "1m"->60;case "5m"->300;case "15m"->900;case "1h"->3600;case "4h"->14400;case "1d"->86400;default->throw new IllegalArgumentException("Unsupported interval: "+interval);};}
    private static Instant expectedClose(Instant open,String interval){return open.plusSeconds(intervalSeconds(interval)).minusMillis(1);}
    private record Candidate(String symbol,String interval,Instant openTime,Instant closeTime) {}
}
package com.crypto.repository;

import com.crypto.domain.Candle;
import com.crypto.shared.SharedMarketSource;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Repository;
import org.springframework.jdbc.core.JdbcTemplate;
import org.springframework.jdbc.core.RowMapper;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Pageable;
import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.sql.Timestamp;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.*;

/** FIX-153: local candle facade in OFF; shared reads remain a rollback capability.
 * Exact predicates/order are retained. Candle is a DTO; SQL migrations own its schema.
 * Runtime and backfill write policies are deliberately distinct. */
@Repository
public class CandleRepository {
    private static final org.slf4j.Logger log=org.slf4j.LoggerFactory.getLogger(CandleRepository.class);
    private final SharedMarketSource source;
    private final JdbcTemplate local;
    public CandleRepository(SharedMarketSource source, JdbcTemplate local) { this.source=source; this.local=local; }
    public static final RowMapper<Candle> MAPPER = (r,n) -> Candle.builder()
        .id(r.getLong("id")).symbol(r.getString("symbol")).intervalCode(r.getString("interval_code"))
        .openTime(r.getTimestamp("open_time").toInstant()).closeTime(r.getTimestamp("close_time").toInstant())
        .openPrice(r.getBigDecimal("open_price")).highPrice(r.getBigDecimal("high_price"))
        .lowPrice(r.getBigDecimal("low_price")).closePrice(r.getBigDecimal("close_price"))
        .volume(r.getBigDecimal("volume")).quoteAssetVolume(r.getBigDecimal("quote_asset_volume"))
        .numberOfTrades(r.getObject("number_of_trades",Long.class))
        .takerBuyBaseVolume(r.getBigDecimal("taker_buy_base_volume"))
        .takerBuyQuoteVolume(r.getBigDecimal("taker_buy_quote_volume")).closed(r.getBoolean("closed")).build();
    private List<Candle> read(String tail,Object... args) {
        Object[] converted=Arrays.stream(args).map(v->v instanceof Instant i ? Timestamp.from(i) : v).toArray();
        List<Candle> rows=source.reader().query("SELECT * FROM candle WHERE "+tail,MAPPER,converted);
        com.crypto.shared.CandleInputAudit.capture(tail,rows);
        return rows;
    }
    private String page(Pageable p) { return p.isUnpaged()? "" : " LIMIT "+p.getPageSize()+" OFFSET "+p.getOffset(); }
    public Optional<Candle> findBySymbolAndIntervalCodeAndOpenTime(String s,String i,Instant t) {
        return read("symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND open_time=?",s,i,t).stream().findFirst();
    }
    public List<Candle> findTop500BySymbolAndIntervalCodeOrderByOpenTimeDesc(String s,String i) {
        return read("symbol=? AND interval_code=? ORDER BY open_time DESC LIMIT 500",s,i);
    }
    public List<Candle> findTop200BySymbolAndIntervalCodeAndClosedTrueOrderByOpenTimeDesc(String s,String i) {
        return read("symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND closed=1 ORDER BY open_time DESC LIMIT 200",s,i);
    }
    public List<Candle> findClosedCandles(String s,String i,Pageable p) {
        return read("symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND closed=1 ORDER BY open_time DESC"+page(p),s,i);
    }
    public List<Candle> findClosedCandlesAtOrBefore(String s,String i,Instant t,Pageable p) {
        return read("symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND closed=1 AND open_time<=? ORDER BY open_time DESC"+page(p),s,i,t);
    }
    public List<Candle> findClosedCandlesClosedAtOrBefore(String s,String i,Instant t,Pageable p) {
        return read("symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND closed=1 AND close_time<=? ORDER BY open_time DESC"+page(p),s,i,t);
    }
    public List<Candle> findBySymbolAndIntervalCodeAndClosedTrueAndOpenTimeBetweenOrderByOpenTimeAsc(String s,String i,Instant a,Instant b) {
        return read("symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND closed=1 AND open_time BETWEEN ? AND ? ORDER BY open_time ASC",s,i,a,b);
    }
    public List<Candle> findBySymbolAndIntervalCodeAndOpenTimeBetweenOrderByOpenTimeAsc(String s,String i,Instant a,Instant b) {
        return read("symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND open_time BETWEEN ? AND ? ORDER BY open_time ASC",s,i,a,b);
    }
    public List<Candle> findBySymbolAndIntervalCodeAndClosedTrueOrderByOpenTimeAsc(String s,String i) {
        return read("symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND closed=1 ORDER BY open_time ASC",s,i);
    }
    public Optional<Candle> findFirstBySymbolAndIntervalCodeAndClosedTrueOrderByCloseTimeDesc(String s,String i) {
        return read("symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND closed=1 ORDER BY close_time DESC LIMIT 1",s,i).stream().findFirst();
    }
    public Optional<Candle> findFirstBySymbolAndIntervalCodeAndClosedTrueOrderByOpenTimeAsc(String s,String i) {
        return read("symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND closed=1 ORDER BY open_time ASC LIMIT 1",s,i).stream().findFirst();
    }
    public List<String> findDistinctSymbols() { return source.reader().queryForList("SELECT DISTINCT symbol FROM candle ORDER BY symbol",String.class); }
    public long countBySymbolAndIntervalCodeAndClosedTrue(String s,String i) {
        return source.reader().queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM candle WHERE symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND closed=1",Long.class,s,i);
    }
    public List<Candle> findClosedCandlesMissingAnalysisThrough(String s,String i,Instant a,Instant b,Pageable p) {
        // Shared credentials never need access to Trader's indicators/signals. Fetch
        // local identities separately, then paginate AFTER applying the missing test.
        Set<Instant> indicators=new HashSet<>(local.query("SELECT candle_open_time FROM technical_indicator WHERE symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND candle_open_time BETWEEN ? AND ?",
            (r,n)->r.getTimestamp(1).toInstant(),s,i,Timestamp.from(a),Timestamp.from(b)));
        Set<Instant> signals=new HashSet<>(local.query("SELECT candle_open_time FROM trade_signal WHERE symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND candle_open_time BETWEEN ? AND ?",
            (r,n)->r.getTimestamp(1).toInstant(),s,i,Timestamp.from(a),Timestamp.from(b)));
        long started=System.nanoTime();
        var candles=findBySymbolAndIntervalCodeAndClosedTrueAndOpenTimeBetweenOrderByOpenTimeAsc(s,i,a,b);
        long fetchMs=(System.nanoTime()-started)/1_000_000;
        var result=candles.stream()
            .filter(c->!indicators.contains(c.getOpenTime()) || !signals.contains(c.getOpenTime()))
            .skip(p.isUnpaged()?0:p.getOffset()).limit(p.isUnpaged()?Long.MAX_VALUE:p.getPageSize()).toList();
        // Measurement only. Fetch includes pool acquisition, transfer and mapping;
        // it is not labelled database execution or commit time. Filtering unchanged.
        if(source.enabled()) {
            String detail="fetched="+candles.size()+", returned="+result.size()+", fetchIncludingPoolMs="+fetchMs+", pool="+source.poolPressure();
            log.info("[FIX-132][RECOVERY_SCAN] symbol={}, interval={}, from={}, through={}, {}",s,i,a,b,detail);
            try { local.update("INSERT INTO shared_market_health(component,status,detail) VALUES(?,'MEASURED',?) ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE status=VALUES(status),detail=VALUES(detail)","RECOVERY:"+s+":"+i,detail); }
            catch(RuntimeException unavailable) {log.warn("[FIX-132][RECOVERY_METRIC_NOT_PERSISTED] symbol={}, interval={}",s,i,unavailable);}
        }
        return result;
    }
    public int upsert(String symbol,String intervalCode,Instant openTime,Instant closeTime,
        BigDecimal openPrice,BigDecimal highPrice,BigDecimal lowPrice,BigDecimal closePrice,
        BigDecimal volume,BigDecimal quoteAssetVolume,Long numberOfTrades,BigDecimal takerBuyBaseVolume,
        BigDecimal takerBuyQuoteVolume,boolean closed) {
        source.requireLocalWriter();
        return write(symbol,intervalCode,openTime,closeTime,openPrice,highPrice,lowPrice,closePrice,
            volume,quoteAssetVolume,numberOfTrades,takerBuyBaseVolume,takerBuyQuoteVolume,closed,"BACKFILL",null);
    }
    /** FIX-153: result is valid only after the caller's INPUT transaction commits.
     * Token read-back compares immutable receipt identity, never a racy pre-read of closed state. */
    public Transition upsertRuntime(String symbol,String intervalCode,Instant openTime,Instant closeTime,
        BigDecimal openPrice,BigDecimal highPrice,BigDecimal lowPrice,BigDecimal closePrice,
        BigDecimal volume,BigDecimal quoteAssetVolume,Long numberOfTrades,BigDecimal takerBuyBaseVolume,
        BigDecimal takerBuyQuoteVolume,boolean closed,String token) {
        source.requireLocalWriter();
        write(symbol,intervalCode,openTime,closeTime,openPrice,highPrice,lowPrice,closePrice,
            volume,quoteAssetVolume,numberOfTrades,takerBuyBaseVolume,takerBuyQuoteVolume,closed,"RUNTIME",closed?token:null);
        return local.queryForObject("SELECT closed,close_time,close_transition_token,ingestion_source FROM candle WHERE symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND open_time=?",
            // Provenance deliberately does not participate here. A legacy NULL row that
            // the live stream closes is a genuine close transition; BACKFILL never writes
            // a token, so it cannot manufacture a dispatchable transition.
            (rs,n)->new Transition(closed && token.equals(rs.getString(3)),
                rs.getTimestamp(2).toInstant(),rs.getString(3),rs.getBoolean(1)),symbol,intervalCode,Timestamp.from(openTime));
    }
    /** `alreadyClosed` is diagnostic only; the matching token is the sole dispatch authority. */
    public record Transition(boolean transitionedToClosed,Instant closeTime,String token,boolean alreadyClosed) {}

    /**
     * Authoritative recovery is intentionally update-only.  Unlike ordinary
     * REST backfill it can never insert an absent identity and it never makes a
     * runtime close token.  This keeps a late recovery from stealing a normal
     * live close admission.
     */
    public boolean finalizeExistingOpenFromAuthoritative(String symbol,String intervalCode,Instant openTime,Instant closeTime,
        BigDecimal openPrice,BigDecimal highPrice,BigDecimal lowPrice,BigDecimal closePrice,
        BigDecimal volume,BigDecimal quoteAssetVolume,Long numberOfTrades,BigDecimal takerBuyBaseVolume,
        BigDecimal takerBuyQuoteVolume) {
        source.requireLocalWriter();
        return local.update("""
            UPDATE candle SET close_time=?,open_price=?,high_price=?,low_price=?,close_price=?,volume=?,
              quote_asset_volume=?,number_of_trades=?,taker_buy_base_volume=?,taker_buy_quote_volume=?,
              closed=1,updated_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6)
            WHERE symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND open_time=? AND closed=0
              AND (ingestion_source IS NULL OR ingestion_source<>'BACKFILL')
            """, Timestamp.from(closeTime),openPrice,highPrice,lowPrice,closePrice,volume,quoteAssetVolume,
            numberOfTrades,takerBuyBaseVolume,takerBuyQuoteVolume,symbol,intervalCode,Timestamp.from(openTime)) == 1;
    }

    /**
     * Exact authoritative gap repair is pure insert-if-absent.  Do not replace
     * this with ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE: a live row may arrive while REST is in
     * flight and must remain untouched so its normal close can own the token.
     */
    public boolean insertAuthoritativeGapBackfill(String symbol,String intervalCode,Instant openTime,Instant closeTime,
        BigDecimal openPrice,BigDecimal highPrice,BigDecimal lowPrice,BigDecimal closePrice,
        BigDecimal volume,BigDecimal quoteAssetVolume,Long numberOfTrades,BigDecimal takerBuyBaseVolume,
        BigDecimal takerBuyQuoteVolume) {
        source.requireLocalWriter();
        try {
            return local.update("""
                INSERT INTO candle(symbol,interval_code,open_time,close_time,open_price,high_price,low_price,close_price,
                    volume,quote_asset_volume,number_of_trades,taker_buy_base_volume,taker_buy_quote_volume,
                    closed,ingestion_source,close_transition_token,created_at,updated_at)
                VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,1,'BACKFILL',NULL,CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6))
                """,symbol,intervalCode,Timestamp.from(openTime),Timestamp.from(closeTime),openPrice,highPrice,lowPrice,
                closePrice,volume,quoteAssetVolume,numberOfTrades,takerBuyBaseVolume,takerBuyQuoteVolume) == 1;
        } catch (org.springframework.dao.DuplicateKeyException duplicate) {
            return false;
        }
    }
    private int write(String symbol,String intervalCode,Instant openTime,Instant closeTime,
        BigDecimal openPrice,BigDecimal highPrice,BigDecimal lowPrice,BigDecimal closePrice,
        BigDecimal volume,BigDecimal quoteAssetVolume,Long numberOfTrades,BigDecimal takerBuyBaseVolume,
        BigDecimal takerBuyQuoteVolume,boolean closed,String provenance,String token) {
        String duplicateClause = "BACKFILL".equals(provenance) ? """
            ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE
                -- REST/backfill is authoritative enough to finish an interrupted open
                -- candle, but never changes a completed candle, provenance or token.
                close_time=IF(closed=1,close_time,VALUES(close_time)),
                open_price=IF(closed=1,open_price,VALUES(open_price)),
                high_price=IF(closed=1,high_price,VALUES(high_price)),
                low_price=IF(closed=1,low_price,VALUES(low_price)),
                close_price=IF(closed=1,close_price,VALUES(close_price)),
                volume=IF(closed=1,volume,VALUES(volume)),
                quote_asset_volume=IF(closed=1,quote_asset_volume,VALUES(quote_asset_volume)),
                number_of_trades=IF(closed=1,number_of_trades,VALUES(number_of_trades)),
                taker_buy_base_volume=IF(closed=1,taker_buy_base_volume,VALUES(taker_buy_base_volume)),
                taker_buy_quote_volume=IF(closed=1,taker_buy_quote_volume,VALUES(taker_buy_quote_volume)),
                updated_at=IF(closed=1,updated_at,CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6)),
                closed=GREATEST(closed,VALUES(closed))
            """ : """
            ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE
                close_time=IF(closed=1,close_time,VALUES(close_time)),
                open_price=IF(closed=1,open_price,VALUES(open_price)),
                high_price=IF(closed=1,high_price,VALUES(high_price)),
                low_price=IF(closed=1,low_price,VALUES(low_price)),
                close_price=IF(closed=1,close_price,VALUES(close_price)),
                volume=IF(closed=1,volume,VALUES(volume)),
                quote_asset_volume=IF(closed=1,quote_asset_volume,VALUES(quote_asset_volume)),
                number_of_trades=IF(closed=1,number_of_trades,VALUES(number_of_trades)),
                taker_buy_base_volume=IF(closed=1,taker_buy_base_volume,VALUES(taker_buy_base_volume)),
                taker_buy_quote_volume=IF(closed=1,taker_buy_quote_volume,VALUES(taker_buy_quote_volume)),
                close_transition_token=IF(closed=0 AND VALUES(closed)=1,VALUES(close_transition_token),close_transition_token),
                updated_at=IF(closed=1,updated_at,CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6)),
                closed=GREATEST(closed,VALUES(closed))
            """;
        // FIX-153: backfill inserts missing identities only, so it cannot fabricate a runtime close.
        return local.update("""
            INSERT INTO candle(symbol,interval_code,open_time,close_time,open_price,high_price,low_price,close_price,
                volume,quote_asset_volume,number_of_trades,taker_buy_base_volume,taker_buy_quote_volume,
                closed,ingestion_source,close_transition_token,created_at,updated_at)
            VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6))
            """ + duplicateClause,symbol,intervalCode,Timestamp.from(openTime),Timestamp.from(closeTime),openPrice,highPrice,lowPrice,
            closePrice,volume,quoteAssetVolume,numberOfTrades,takerBuyBaseVolume,takerBuyQuoteVolume,closed,provenance,token);
    }
}
-- FIX-154: immutable quality evidence belongs to each generated signal.
-- NULL is intentional for signals generated before this migration.
ALTER TABLE trade_signal
    ADD COLUMN data_quality_valid BOOLEAN NULL;

-- Execution-time evidence is append-only: the same reference signal can be
-- assessed repeatedly through different or repeated authority paths.
CREATE TABLE buy_htf_assessment_audit (
    id BIGINT NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
    reference_signal_id BIGINT NOT NULL,
    evaluation_path VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL,
    evaluated_at TIMESTAMP(6) NOT NULL,
    replay_mode BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    one_hour_signal_id BIGINT NULL,
    one_hour_raw_decision VARCHAR(30) NULL,
    one_hour_effective_decision VARCHAR(30) NULL,
    one_hour_candle_close_time TIMESTAMP(6) NULL,
    one_hour_age_seconds BIGINT NULL,
    one_hour_data_quality_valid BOOLEAN NULL,
    one_hour_usability_reason VARCHAR(96) NULL,
    five_minute_signal_id BIGINT NULL,
    five_minute_raw_decision VARCHAR(30) NULL,
    five_minute_effective_decision VARCHAR(30) NULL,
    five_minute_candle_close_time TIMESTAMP(6) NULL,

package com.crypto.service;

import java.util.Comparator;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Locale;

import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;
import org.springframework.transaction.PlatformTransactionManager;
import org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionTemplate;

import com.crypto.client.binance.BinanceMarketDataClient;
import com.crypto.client.binance.dto.BinanceKline;
import com.crypto.domain.Candle;
import com.crypto.repository.CandleRepository;

@Service
public class MarketDataService {
    @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value("#{'${shared-market.mode:OFF}' == 'LIVE'}")
    private boolean sharedMarketEnabled;


    private final BinanceMarketDataClient client;
    private final CandleRepository candleRepository;
    private final TransactionTemplate transaction;

    public MarketDataService(BinanceMarketDataClient client,CandleRepository candleRepository,PlatformTransactionManager manager){
        this.client=client;this.candleRepository=candleRepository;this.transaction=new TransactionTemplate(manager);
    }

    public int importCandles(String symbol, String interval, int limit) {
        if (sharedMarketEnabled) { throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-132: collector owns candle imports"); }
        String normalizedSymbol = symbol.trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
        // Do not trust a potentially skewed local clock to finalise Binance's
        // currently-open REST candle. Read it before klines: a later timestamp
        // could otherwise turn an in-flight response row into a false close.
        java.time.Instant exchangeNow=client.getServerTime();
        List<BinanceKline> klines = client.getKlines(normalizedSymbol, interval, limit);
        // No database transaction is open while Binance is contacted. The local
        // writes remain one transaction once the bounded network calls return.
        Integer upserted=transaction.execute(status->{
            int count=0;
            for (BinanceKline kline : klines) {
                // FIX-153: an in-progress REST candle is not a close event or historical evidence.
                if (kline.closeTime().isAfter(exchangeNow)) continue;
                count += candleRepository.upsert(
                        normalizedSymbol,interval,kline.openTime(),kline.closeTime(),
                        kline.openPrice(),kline.highPrice(),kline.lowPrice(),kline.closePrice(),
                        kline.volume(),kline.quoteAssetVolume(),kline.numberOfTrades(),
                        kline.takerBuyBaseVolume(),kline.takerBuyQuoteVolume(),true);
            }
            return count;
        });
        if(upserted==null)throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-153 local candle import transaction returned no result");
        return upserted;
    }

    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public List<Candle> latestCandles(String symbol, String interval) {
        return candleRepository
                .findTop500BySymbolAndIntervalCodeOrderByOpenTimeDesc(
                        symbol.trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT), interval)
                .stream()
                .sorted(Comparator.comparing(Candle::getOpenTime))
                .toList();
    }

    private Candle toEntity(String symbol, String interval, BinanceKline kline) {
        return Candle.builder()
                .symbol(symbol)
                .intervalCode(interval)
                .openTime(kline.openTime())
                .closeTime(kline.closeTime())
                .openPrice(kline.openPrice())
                .highPrice(kline.highPrice())
                .lowPrice(kline.lowPrice())
                .closePrice(kline.closePrice())
                .volume(kline.volume())
                .quoteAssetVolume(kline.quoteAssetVolume())
                .numberOfTrades(kline.numberOfTrades())
                .takerBuyBaseVolume(kline.takerBuyBaseVolume())
                .takerBuyQuoteVolume(kline.takerBuyQuoteVolume())
                .closed(true)
                .build();
    }
}
package com.crypto.service;

import com.crypto.administration.service.CoinConfigurationService;
import com.crypto.client.config.binance.BinanceMarketDataProperties;
import com.crypto.config.TradingProperties;
import com.crypto.domain.TechnicalIndicator;
import com.crypto.domain.TradeSignal;
