import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import com.crypto.repository.TradeSignalRepository;
import org.springframework.boot.ApplicationArguments;
import org.springframework.boot.ApplicationRunner;
import org.springframework.core.annotation.Order;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;

import java.util.LinkedHashSet;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Set;

/**
 * Ensures every configured symbol and interval has enough historical candles
 * when the application starts. Existing candles are preserved and only missing
 * Binance rows are inserted by MarketDataService.
 */
@Component
@Order(10)
@RequiredArgsConstructor
@Slf4j
public class MarketDataBootstrapService implements ApplicationRunner {
    @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value("#{'${shared-market.mode:OFF}' == 'LIVE'}")
    private boolean sharedMarketEnabled;
    @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value("${shared-market.mode:OFF}")
    private String marketMode="OFF";


    private static final int REQUIRED_CANDLES = 300;

    private final BinanceMarketDataProperties marketDataProperties;
    private final TradingProperties tradingProperties;
    private final CoinConfigurationService coinConfigurationService;
    private final MarketDataService marketDataService;
    private final TechnicalIndicatorService technicalIndicatorService;
    private final AnalysisService analysisService;
    private final PaperTradingService paperTradingService;
    private final TradeSignalRepository tradeSignalRepository;

    @Override
    public void run(ApplicationArguments args) {
        if (sharedMarketEnabled) { return; }
        if (!marketDataProperties.isEnabled()
                || !marketDataProperties.getHistorical().isEnabled()) {
            log.info("Historical candle bootstrap is disabled");
            return;
        }

        List<String> symbols = selectSymbols();
        List<String> intervals = selectIntervals();
        int limit = Math.max(
                REQUIRED_CANDLES,
                Math.min(1000, marketDataProperties.getHistorical().getLimit())
        );

        for (String symbol : symbols) {
            for (String interval : intervals) {
                bootstrap(symbol, interval, limit);
            }
        }
    }


    public void bootstrapSymbol(String rawSymbol) {
        if (sharedMarketEnabled) { return; }
        if (!marketDataProperties.isEnabled()
                || !marketDataProperties.getHistorical().isEnabled()) {
            log.info("Historical candle bootstrap skipped for {} because historical loading is disabled", rawSymbol);
            return;
        }

        String symbol = rawSymbol == null ? "" : rawSymbol.trim().toUpperCase();
        if (symbol.isBlank()) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Symbol is required");
        }

        int limit = Math.max(
                REQUIRED_CANDLES,
                Math.min(1000, marketDataProperties.getHistorical().getLimit())
        );
        for (String interval : selectIntervals()) {
            bootstrap(symbol, interval, limit);
        }
    }

    private void bootstrap(String symbol, String interval, int limit) {
        try {
            int inserted = marketDataService.importCandles(symbol, interval, limit);

            log.info(
                    "Historical bootstrap completed: symbol={}, interval={}, inserted={}",
                    symbol,
                    interval,
                    inserted
            );

            // FIX-153: bootstrap is data only. An imported close must never seed a signal
            // or execute a STARTUP trade; only this trader's runtime closes qualify in OFF.
            if("OFF".equals(marketMode)){
                log.info("[FIX-153][BACKFILL_IMPORT_RETURNED] symbol={}, interval={}, inserted={}, analysis=false, execution=false",symbol,interval,inserted);
                return;
            }
            TechnicalIndicator indicator = technicalIndicatorService
                    .calculateAndPersist(symbol, interval, null)
                    .orElse(null);

            if (indicator == null) {
                log.warn(
                        "Initial indicator skipped: symbol={}, interval={}, reason=fewer than 210 closed candles",
                        symbol,
                        interval
                );
                return;
            }

            // FIX-072: bootstrap can overlap with the live candle-close worker during startup.
            // Never regenerate or re-execute a signal that Production already persisted for this
            // exact symbol/interval/candle; the unique key remains the final database safety net.
            if (tradeSignalRepository.existsBySymbolAndIntervalAndCandleOpenTime(
                    indicator.getSymbol(), indicator.getIntervalCode(), indicator.getCandleOpenTime())) {

        String intervalCode = kline.path("i")
                .asText()
                .trim();

        Instant openTime = Instant.ofEpochMilli(
                kline.path("t").asLong()
        );

        Instant closeTime = Instant.ofEpochMilli(
                kline.path("T").asLong()
        );

        boolean closed = kline.path("x")
                .asBoolean(false);
        BigDecimal livePrice = MarketPriceEventService.normalizePrice(decimal(kline, "c"));

        // FIX-153: an absent Binance event timestamp is not equivalent to a fresh receipt.
        Instant observedAt = data.path("E").asLong(0L)>0 ? Instant.ofEpochMilli(data.path("E").asLong()) : null;
        boolean canonical="1m".equals(intervalCode);
        String token=java.util.UUID.randomUUID().toString();
        java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicReference<CandleRepository.Transition> transition=new java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicReference<>();
        java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicReference<Long> priceId=new java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicReference<>();
        long started=System.nanoTime();
        try {
            transactions.process(symbol,intervalCode,openTime,observedAt,livePrice,
                ()->{
                    transition.set(candleRepository.upsertRuntime(symbol,intervalCode,openTime,closeTime,
                        decimal(kline,"o"),decimal(kline,"h"),decimal(kline,"l"),livePrice,
                        decimal(kline,"v"),decimal(kline,"q"),kline.path("n").asLong(),decimal(kline,"V"),decimal(kline,"Q"),closed,token));
                    if(canonical)priceId.set(marketPriceEventService.recordPending(symbol,livePrice,observedAt));
                },
                canonical ? ()->{if(priceId.get()!=null)livePositionProtectionService.onPrice(symbol,livePrice,observedAt,priceId.get());} : null,
                canonical ? ()->{if(priceId.get()!=null)priceMoveMonitorService.onPrice(symbol,livePrice,observedAt);} : null,
                closed ? ()->{var result=transition.get();if(result!=null && result.transitionedToClosed())
                    eventPublisher.publishEvent(new CandleClosedEvent(symbol,intervalCode,openTime,result.closeTime()));} : null);

            var completedTransition=transition.get();
            if(closed && completedTransition!=null && !completedTransition.transitionedToClosed()) {
                com.crypto.local.LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.count(com.crypto.local.LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.Event.DUPLICATE_CLOSE);
                log.info("[FIX-153][DUPLICATE_CLOSE] receipt={}, symbol={}, interval={}, openUtc={}, existingToken={}",
                    token,symbol,intervalCode,openTime,completedTransition.token());
                // This audit is intentionally after the ingestion transaction and
                // fail-safe: an audit problem must never make a Binance receipt fail.
                if(reconciliationAudit!=null)try {reconciliationAudit.markLateLiveClose(symbol,intervalCode,openTime);}
                catch(RuntimeException ignored){log.warn("[FIX-153][LATE_LIVE_CLOSE_AUDIT_FAILED] symbol={}, interval={}, openUtc={}",symbol,intervalCode,openTime);}
            } else if(!closed && completedTransition!=null && completedTransition.alreadyClosed()) {
                com.crypto.local.LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.count(com.crypto.local.LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.Event.LATE_OPEN_IGNORED);
                log.info("[FIX-153][LATE_OPEN_IGNORED] receipt={}, symbol={}, interval={}, openUtc={}",token,symbol,intervalCode,openTime);
            }

            log.debug("[FIX-153][KLINE_PHASES_RETURNED] receipt={}, event={}, symbol={}, interval={}, openUtc={}, observedUtc={}, transitionedToClosed={}, elapsedIncludingTransactionsMs={}; protection outcome is in price evidence",
                token,priceId.get(),symbol,intervalCode,openTime,observedAt,transition.get()!=null&&transition.get().transitionedToClosed(),(System.nanoTime()-started)/1_000_000);
        }catch(RuntimeException failure){
            com.crypto.local.LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.count(com.crypto.local.LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.Event.PIPELINE_FAILED_OR_UNCONFIRMED);
            log.error("[FIX-153][KLINE_PHASE_FAILED_OR_UNCONFIRMED] receipt={}, event={}, symbol={}, interval={}, openUtc={}; inspect durable evidence, do not infer no effects",token,priceId.get(),symbol,intervalCode,openTime,failure);
            throw failure;
        }finally{if(priceId.get()!=null)marketPriceEventService.release(priceId.get());}

        return closed;
        } finally {if(drain!=null)drain.completeInput();}
    }

package com.crypto.local;

import com.crypto.administration.service.CoinConfigurationService;
import com.crypto.indicator.event.*;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
import org.springframework.scheduling.annotation.Scheduled;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value;
import org.springframework.jdbc.core.JdbcTemplate;
import org.slf4j.*;
import java.time.*;
import java.sql.Timestamp;
import java.util.*;

/** FIX-153: local durable candles close the commit-to-memory-dispatch crash window.
 * BACKFILL is never admitted. Legacy NULL provenance is eligible only when the
 * live stream wrote a close-transition token.
 * Small lookback and one admission per idle lane keep detector below live work. */
@Component
public class LocalClosedCandleDetector {
    private static final Logger log=LoggerFactory.getLogger(LocalClosedCandleDetector.class);
    private final JdbcTemplate jdbc;private final CoinConfigurationService coins;
    private final CandleAnalysisDispatcher dispatcher;private final CandleAnalysisExecutionCoordinator coordination;
    private final com.crypto.client.config.binance.BinanceMarketDataProperties properties;
    @Value("${shared-market.mode:OFF}") private String mode;
    @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired(required=false) private ExchangeClockSynchronizer exchangeClock;
    @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired(required=false) private CandleAnalysisOutcomeStore outcomes;
    private int nextLane;
    private final Map<String,Retry> retries=new HashMap<>();
    private final Map<String,Instant> analysedUntil=new HashMap<>();
    public LocalClosedCandleDetector(JdbcTemplate jdbc,CoinConfigurationService coins,CandleAnalysisDispatcher dispatcher,CandleAnalysisExecutionCoordinator coordination,com.crypto.client.config.binance.BinanceMarketDataProperties properties){
        this.jdbc=new JdbcTemplate(Objects.requireNonNull(jdbc.getDataSource()));this.jdbc.setQueryTimeout(2);
        this.coins=coins;this.dispatcher=dispatcher;this.coordination=coordination;this.properties=properties;
    }
    @Scheduled(initialDelay=5000,fixedDelayString="${local-candle.detector-delay-ms:2000}")
    public void detect(){
        if(!"OFF".equals(mode))return;
        Instant localNow=Instant.now();Instant now=exchangeClock==null?localNow:exchangeClock.correctJvm(localNow);long deadline=System.nanoTime()+java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit.SECONDS.toNanos(2);
        try {
            var symbols=coins.enabledSymbols();var intervals=properties.getIntervals();
            int total=symbols.size()*intervals.size();
            for(int checked=0;checked<total;checked++){
                if(System.nanoTime()>deadline)return;
                // FIX-153: rotate across bounded passes so a slow first lane cannot starve later symbols.
                int lane=Math.floorMod(nextLane++,total);
                String symbol=symbols.get(lane/intervals.size()),interval=intervals.get(lane%intervals.size());
                if(!dispatcher.idle(symbol,interval))continue;
                long lookback=Math.max(180,com.crypto.shared.SharedEventPolicy.closeGraceSeconds(interval)*2L);
                // Index is on open_time: include one full interval before the close-age lookback.
                Instant lowerOpen=now.minusSeconds(intervalSeconds(interval)+lookback);
                var candidates=jdbc.query("SELECT open_time,close_time FROM candle WHERE symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND closed=1 AND close_transition_token IS NOT NULL AND (ingestion_source IS NULL OR ingestion_source<>'BACKFILL') AND open_time>=? AND close_time<=? ORDER BY open_time ASC LIMIT 4",
                    (rs,n)->new CandleClosedEvent(symbol,interval,rs.getTimestamp(1).toInstant(),rs.getTimestamp(2).toInstant()),symbol,interval,Timestamp.from(lowerOpen),Timestamp.from(now));
                for(var event:candidates){
                    String retryKey=key(event);
                    Instant knownAnalysedUntil=analysedUntil.get(retryKey);
                    if(knownAnalysedUntil!=null && knownAnalysedUntil.isAfter(now))continue;
                    Retry retry=retries.get(retryKey);
                    if(retry!=null && retry.nextAttempt().isAfter(now))continue;
                    // A healthy analysis is terminal for detector purposes. Do this
                    // before dispatcher admission so ordinary completed candles never
                    // become retry/expiry noise.
                    if(!missing(event)){retries.remove(retryKey);analysedUntil.put(retryKey,eligibilityEndsAfter(event,lookback));continue;}
                    // No database fence; existing striped candle lock covers only check+handoff.
                    var acquired=coordination.tryLock(symbol,interval,event.openTime());
                    if(acquired.isEmpty())continue;
                    try(var ignored=acquired.get()){
                        if(dispatcher.submitDetected(event,()->missing(event))){
                            // Admission is not completion. Retain bounded state until
                            // durable signal/work evidence appears on a later pass.
                            // Every re-admission consumes the same bounded retry budget;
                            // a repeatedly failing worker must not execute forever.
                            recordAdmission(retryKey,event,now,lookback);
                            break;
                        }
                        defer(retryKey,event,now,"NOT_ADMITTED");
                    }
                }
            }
            for(var iterator=retries.entrySet().iterator();iterator.hasNext();){
                var entry=iterator.next();
                // Retention is strictly after the SQL lower-open-time boundary.
                // Therefore pruning cannot make a still-detectable candle restart
                // with a fresh retry budget.
                if(entry.getValue().retainUntil().isBefore(now)){
                    log.info("[FIX-153][DETECTOR_RETRY_PRUNED] candle={}, attempts={}, reason=NO_LONGER_ELIGIBLE",entry.getKey(),entry.getValue().attempts());
                    iterator.remove();
                }
            }
            analysedUntil.entrySet().removeIf(e->!e.getValue().isAfter(now));
        }catch(RuntimeException failure){LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.count(LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.Event.DETECTOR_FAILED);
            log.warn("[FIX-153][DETECTOR_FAILED] no checkpoint advanced; next bounded pass may retry",failure);}
    }
    private static String key(CandleClosedEvent e){return e.symbol()+"|"+e.intervalCode()+"|"+e.openTime();}
    private void defer(String key,CandleClosedEvent event,Instant now,String reason){
        Retry prior=retries.get(key);int attempts=prior==null?1:prior.attempts()+1;
        long delay=Math.min(60,1L<<Math.min(6,attempts));
        long lookback=Math.max(180,com.crypto.shared.SharedEventPolicy.closeGraceSeconds(event.intervalCode())*2L);
        Instant retryDeadline=event.closeTime().plusSeconds(lookback);
        Instant retainUntil=eligibilityEndsAfter(event,lookback);
        if(attempts>6 || !retryDeadline.isAfter(now)){
            // Keep a terminal marker until this candle has aged out of the SQL
            // lookback. Removing it immediately would restart the retry budget.
            retries.put(key,new Retry(attempts,Instant.MAX,retryDeadline,retainUntil));
            recordExpired(event,reason);
            log.warn("[FIX-153][DETECTOR_GAP_EXPIRED] candle={}, attempts={}, reason={}",key,attempts,reason);return;
        }
        retries.put(key,new Retry(attempts,now.plusSeconds(delay),retryDeadline,retainUntil));
        log.info("[FIX-153][DETECTOR_RETRY_DEFERRED] candle={}, attempts={}, nextUtc={}, reason={}",key,attempts,now.plusSeconds(delay),reason);
    }
    private void recordAdmission(String key,CandleClosedEvent event,Instant now,long lookback){
        Retry prior=retries.get(key);int attempts=prior==null?1:prior.attempts()+1;
        Instant retryDeadline=event.closeTime().plusSeconds(lookback);
        Instant retainUntil=eligibilityEndsAfter(event,lookback);
        if(attempts>6 || !retryDeadline.isAfter(now)){
            // See defer(): this marker is deliberately retained until the query
            // can no longer rediscover this candle.
            retries.put(key,new Retry(attempts,Instant.MAX,retryDeadline,retainUntil));
            recordExpired(event,"ADMISSION_RETRY_LIMIT");
            log.warn("[FIX-153][DETECTOR_GAP_EXPIRED] candle={}, attempts={}, reason=ADMISSION_RETRY_LIMIT",key,attempts);
            return;
        }
        long delay=retryDelaySeconds(attempts);
        retries.put(key,new Retry(attempts,now.plusSeconds(delay),retryDeadline,retainUntil));
        log.info("[FIX-153][DETECTOR_ADMITTED_AWAITING_EVIDENCE] candle={}, attempts={}, nextUtc={}",key,attempts,now.plusSeconds(delay));
    }
    /** Strictly after the candidate predicate: open_time >= now - interval - lookback. */
    private static Instant eligibilityEndsAfter(CandleClosedEvent event,long lookback){
        return event.openTime().plusSeconds(intervalSeconds(event.intervalCode())+lookback+1);
    }
    private static long retryDelaySeconds(int attempts){return Math.min(60,1L<<Math.min(6,attempts));}
    private record Retry(int attempts,Instant nextAttempt,Instant retryDeadline,Instant retainUntil) {}
    private static long intervalSeconds(String interval){
        return switch(interval){case "1m"->60;case "5m"->300;case "15m"->900;case "1h"->3600;case "4h"->14400;case "1d"->86400;
            default->throw new IllegalArgumentException("FIX-153 unsupported detector interval: "+interval);};
    }
    private boolean missing(CandleClosedEvent e){
        if(outcomes!=null && outcomes.exists(e.symbol(),e.intervalCode(),e.openTime()))return false;
        return jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT (EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM trade_signal WHERE symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND candle_open_time=?) OR EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM signal_processing_work WHERE symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND candle_open_time=?))",Integer.class,
            e.symbol(),e.intervalCode(),Timestamp.from(e.openTime()),e.symbol(),e.intervalCode(),Timestamp.from(e.openTime()))==0;
    }
    private void recordExpired(CandleClosedEvent event,String reason){
        if(outcomes!=null)outcomes.record(event.symbol(),event.intervalCode(),event.openTime(),"ANALYSIS_EXPIRED",reason,null,"LOCAL_DETECTOR");
    }
}

package com.crypto.service;

import java.util.Comparator;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Locale;

import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;
import org.springframework.transaction.PlatformTransactionManager;
import org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionTemplate;

import com.crypto.client.binance.BinanceMarketDataClient;
import com.crypto.client.binance.dto.BinanceKline;
import com.crypto.domain.Candle;
import com.crypto.repository.CandleRepository;

@Service
public class MarketDataService {
    @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value("#{'${shared-market.mode:OFF}' == 'LIVE'}")
    private boolean sharedMarketEnabled;


    private final BinanceMarketDataClient client;
    private final CandleRepository candleRepository;
    private final TransactionTemplate transaction;

    public MarketDataService(BinanceMarketDataClient client,CandleRepository candleRepository,PlatformTransactionManager manager){
        this.client=client;this.candleRepository=candleRepository;this.transaction=new TransactionTemplate(manager);
    }

    public int importCandles(String symbol, String interval, int limit) {
        if (sharedMarketEnabled) { throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-132: collector owns candle imports"); }
        String normalizedSymbol = symbol.trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
        // Do not trust a potentially skewed local clock to finalise Binance's
        // currently-open REST candle. Read it before klines: a later timestamp
        // could otherwise turn an in-flight response row into a false close.
        java.time.Instant exchangeNow=client.getServerTime();
        List<BinanceKline> klines = client.getKlines(normalizedSymbol, interval, limit);
        // No database transaction is open while Binance is contacted. The local
        // writes remain one transaction once the bounded network calls return.
        Integer upserted=transaction.execute(status->{
            int count=0;
            for (BinanceKline kline : klines) {
                // FIX-153: an in-progress REST candle is not a close event or historical evidence.
                if (kline.closeTime().isAfter(exchangeNow)) continue;
                count += candleRepository.upsert(
                        normalizedSymbol,interval,kline.openTime(),kline.closeTime(),
                        kline.openPrice(),kline.highPrice(),kline.lowPrice(),kline.closePrice(),
                        kline.volume(),kline.quoteAssetVolume(),kline.numberOfTrades(),
                        kline.takerBuyBaseVolume(),kline.takerBuyQuoteVolume(),true);
            }
            return count;
        });
        if(upserted==null)throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-153 local candle import transaction returned no result");
        return upserted;
    }

    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public List<Candle> latestCandles(String symbol, String interval) {
        return candleRepository
                .findTop500BySymbolAndIntervalCodeOrderByOpenTimeDesc(
                        symbol.trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT), interval)
                .stream()
                .sorted(Comparator.comparing(Candle::getOpenTime))
                .toList();
    }

    private Candle toEntity(String symbol, String interval, BinanceKline kline) {
        return Candle.builder()
                .symbol(symbol)
                .intervalCode(interval)
                .openTime(kline.openTime())
                .closeTime(kline.closeTime())
                .openPrice(kline.openPrice())
                .highPrice(kline.highPrice())
                .lowPrice(kline.lowPrice())
                .closePrice(kline.closePrice())
                .volume(kline.volume())
                .quoteAssetVolume(kline.quoteAssetVolume())
                .numberOfTrades(kline.numberOfTrades())
                .takerBuyBaseVolume(kline.takerBuyBaseVolume())
                .takerBuyQuoteVolume(kline.takerBuyQuoteVolume())
                .closed(true)
                .build();
    }
}
package com.crypto.service;

import com.crypto.administration.service.CoinConfigurationService;
import com.crypto.client.config.binance.BinanceMarketDataProperties;
import com.crypto.config.TradingProperties;
import com.crypto.domain.TechnicalIndicator;
import com.crypto.domain.TradeSignal;
import com.crypto.indicator.service.TechnicalIndicatorService;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import com.crypto.repository.TradeSignalRepository;
import org.springframework.boot.ApplicationArguments;
import org.springframework.boot.ApplicationRunner;
import org.springframework.core.annotation.Order;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;

import java.util.LinkedHashSet;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Set;

/**
 * Ensures every configured symbol and interval has enough historical candles
 * when the application starts. Existing candles are preserved and only missing
 * Binance rows are inserted by MarketDataService.
 */
@Component
@Order(10)
@RequiredArgsConstructor
@Slf4j
public class MarketDataBootstrapService implements ApplicationRunner {
    @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value("#{'${shared-market.mode:OFF}' == 'LIVE'}")
    private boolean sharedMarketEnabled;
    @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value("${shared-market.mode:OFF}")
    private String marketMode="OFF";


    private static final int REQUIRED_CANDLES = 300;

    private final BinanceMarketDataProperties marketDataProperties;
    private final TradingProperties tradingProperties;
    private final CoinConfigurationService coinConfigurationService;
    private final MarketDataService marketDataService;
    private final TechnicalIndicatorService technicalIndicatorService;
    private final AnalysisService analysisService;
    private final PaperTradingService paperTradingService;
    private final TradeSignalRepository tradeSignalRepository;

    @Override
    public void run(ApplicationArguments args) {
        if (sharedMarketEnabled) { return; }
        if (!marketDataProperties.isEnabled()
                || !marketDataProperties.getHistorical().isEnabled()) {
            log.info("Historical candle bootstrap is disabled");
            return;
        }

        List<String> symbols = selectSymbols();
        List<String> intervals = selectIntervals();
        int limit = Math.max(
                REQUIRED_CANDLES,
                Math.min(1000, marketDataProperties.getHistorical().getLimit())
        );

        for (String symbol : symbols) {
            for (String interval : intervals) {
                bootstrap(symbol, interval, limit);
            }
        }
    }


    public void bootstrapSymbol(String rawSymbol) {
        if (sharedMarketEnabled) { return; }
        if (!marketDataProperties.isEnabled()
                || !marketDataProperties.getHistorical().isEnabled()) {
            log.info("Historical candle bootstrap skipped for {} because historical loading is disabled", rawSymbol);
            return;
        }

        String symbol = rawSymbol == null ? "" : rawSymbol.trim().toUpperCase();
        if (symbol.isBlank()) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Symbol is required");
        }

        int limit = Math.max(
                REQUIRED_CANDLES,
                Math.min(1000, marketDataProperties.getHistorical().getLimit())
        );
        for (String interval : selectIntervals()) {
            bootstrap(symbol, interval, limit);
        }
    }

    private void bootstrap(String symbol, String interval, int limit) {
        try {
            int inserted = marketDataService.importCandles(symbol, interval, limit);

            log.info(
                    "Historical bootstrap completed: symbol={}, interval={}, inserted={}",
                    symbol,
                    interval,
                    inserted
            );

            // FIX-153: bootstrap is data only. An imported close must never seed a signal
            // or execute a STARTUP trade; only this trader's runtime closes qualify in OFF.
            if("OFF".equals(marketMode)){
                log.info("[FIX-153][BACKFILL_IMPORT_RETURNED] symbol={}, interval={}, inserted={}, analysis=false, execution=false",symbol,interval,inserted);
                return;
            }
            TechnicalIndicator indicator = technicalIndicatorService
                    .calculateAndPersist(symbol, interval, null)
                    .orElse(null);

            if (indicator == null) {
                log.warn(
                        "Initial indicator skipped: symbol={}, interval={}, reason=fewer than 210 closed candles",
                        symbol,
                        interval
                );
                return;
            }

            // FIX-072: bootstrap can overlap with the live candle-close worker during startup.
            // Never regenerate or re-execute a signal that Production already persisted for this
            // exact symbol/interval/candle; the unique key remains the final database safety net.
            if (tradeSignalRepository.existsBySymbolAndIntervalAndCandleOpenTime(
                    indicator.getSymbol(), indicator.getIntervalCode(), indicator.getCandleOpenTime())) {

        String intervalCode = kline.path("i")
                .asText()
                .trim();

        Instant openTime = Instant.ofEpochMilli(
                kline.path("t").asLong()
        );

        Instant closeTime = Instant.ofEpochMilli(
                kline.path("T").asLong()
        );

        boolean closed = kline.path("x")
                .asBoolean(false);
        BigDecimal livePrice = MarketPriceEventService.normalizePrice(decimal(kline, "c"));

        // FIX-153: an absent Binance event timestamp is not equivalent to a fresh receipt.
        Instant observedAt = data.path("E").asLong(0L)>0 ? Instant.ofEpochMilli(data.path("E").asLong()) : null;
        boolean canonical="1m".equals(intervalCode);
        String token=java.util.UUID.randomUUID().toString();
        java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicReference<CandleRepository.Transition> transition=new java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicReference<>();
        java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicReference<Long> priceId=new java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicReference<>();
        long started=System.nanoTime();
        try {
            transactions.process(symbol,intervalCode,openTime,observedAt,livePrice,
                ()->{
                    transition.set(candleRepository.upsertRuntime(symbol,intervalCode,openTime,closeTime,
                        decimal(kline,"o"),decimal(kline,"h"),decimal(kline,"l"),livePrice,
                        decimal(kline,"v"),decimal(kline,"q"),kline.path("n").asLong(),decimal(kline,"V"),decimal(kline,"Q"),closed,token));
                    if(canonical)priceId.set(marketPriceEventService.recordPending(symbol,livePrice,observedAt));
                },
                canonical ? ()->{if(priceId.get()!=null)livePositionProtectionService.onPrice(symbol,livePrice,observedAt,priceId.get());} : null,
                canonical ? ()->{if(priceId.get()!=null)priceMoveMonitorService.onPrice(symbol,livePrice,observedAt);} : null,
                closed ? ()->{var result=transition.get();if(result!=null && result.transitionedToClosed())
                    eventPublisher.publishEvent(new CandleClosedEvent(symbol,intervalCode,openTime,result.closeTime()));} : null);

            var completedTransition=transition.get();
            if(closed && completedTransition!=null && !completedTransition.transitionedToClosed()) {
                com.crypto.local.LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.count(com.crypto.local.LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.Event.DUPLICATE_CLOSE);
                log.info("[FIX-153][DUPLICATE_CLOSE] receipt={}, symbol={}, interval={}, openUtc={}, existingToken={}",
                    token,symbol,intervalCode,openTime,completedTransition.token());
                // This audit is intentionally after the ingestion transaction and
                // fail-safe: an audit problem must never make a Binance receipt fail.
                if(reconciliationAudit!=null)try {reconciliationAudit.markLateLiveClose(symbol,intervalCode,openTime);}
                catch(RuntimeException ignored){log.warn("[FIX-153][LATE_LIVE_CLOSE_AUDIT_FAILED] symbol={}, interval={}, openUtc={}",symbol,intervalCode,openTime);}
            } else if(!closed && completedTransition!=null && completedTransition.alreadyClosed()) {
                com.crypto.local.LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.count(com.crypto.local.LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.Event.LATE_OPEN_IGNORED);
                log.info("[FIX-153][LATE_OPEN_IGNORED] receipt={}, symbol={}, interval={}, openUtc={}",token,symbol,intervalCode,openTime);
            }

            log.debug("[FIX-153][KLINE_PHASES_RETURNED] receipt={}, event={}, symbol={}, interval={}, openUtc={}, observedUtc={}, transitionedToClosed={}, elapsedIncludingTransactionsMs={}; protection outcome is in price evidence",
                token,priceId.get(),symbol,intervalCode,openTime,observedAt,transition.get()!=null&&transition.get().transitionedToClosed(),(System.nanoTime()-started)/1_000_000);
        }catch(RuntimeException failure){
            com.crypto.local.LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.count(com.crypto.local.LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.Event.PIPELINE_FAILED_OR_UNCONFIRMED);
            log.error("[FIX-153][KLINE_PHASE_FAILED_OR_UNCONFIRMED] receipt={}, event={}, symbol={}, interval={}, openUtc={}; inspect durable evidence, do not infer no effects",token,priceId.get(),symbol,intervalCode,openTime,failure);
            throw failure;
        }finally{if(priceId.get()!=null)marketPriceEventService.release(priceId.get());}

        return closed;
        } finally {if(drain!=null)drain.completeInput();}
    }

package com.crypto.local;

import com.crypto.administration.service.CoinConfigurationService;
import com.crypto.indicator.event.*;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
import org.springframework.scheduling.annotation.Scheduled;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value;
import org.springframework.jdbc.core.JdbcTemplate;
import org.slf4j.*;
import java.time.*;
import java.sql.Timestamp;
import java.util.*;

/** FIX-153: local durable candles close the commit-to-memory-dispatch crash window.
 * BACKFILL is never admitted. Legacy NULL provenance is eligible only when the
 * live stream wrote a close-transition token.
 * Small lookback and one admission per idle lane keep detector below live work. */
@Component
public class LocalClosedCandleDetector {
    private static final Logger log=LoggerFactory.getLogger(LocalClosedCandleDetector.class);
    private final JdbcTemplate jdbc;private final CoinConfigurationService coins;
    private final CandleAnalysisDispatcher dispatcher;private final CandleAnalysisExecutionCoordinator coordination;
    private final com.crypto.client.config.binance.BinanceMarketDataProperties properties;
    @Value("${shared-market.mode:OFF}") private String mode;
    @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired(required=false) private ExchangeClockSynchronizer exchangeClock;
    @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired(required=false) private CandleAnalysisOutcomeStore outcomes;
    private int nextLane;
    private final Map<String,Retry> retries=new HashMap<>();
    private final Map<String,Instant> analysedUntil=new HashMap<>();
    public LocalClosedCandleDetector(JdbcTemplate jdbc,CoinConfigurationService coins,CandleAnalysisDispatcher dispatcher,CandleAnalysisExecutionCoordinator coordination,com.crypto.client.config.binance.BinanceMarketDataProperties properties){
        this.jdbc=new JdbcTemplate(Objects.requireNonNull(jdbc.getDataSource()));this.jdbc.setQueryTimeout(2);
        this.coins=coins;this.dispatcher=dispatcher;this.coordination=coordination;this.properties=properties;
    }
    @Scheduled(initialDelay=5000,fixedDelayString="${local-candle.detector-delay-ms:2000}")
    public void detect(){
        if(!"OFF".equals(mode))return;
        Instant localNow=Instant.now();Instant now=exchangeClock==null?localNow:exchangeClock.correctJvm(localNow);long deadline=System.nanoTime()+java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit.SECONDS.toNanos(2);
        try {
            var symbols=coins.enabledSymbols();var intervals=properties.getIntervals();
            int total=symbols.size()*intervals.size();
            for(int checked=0;checked<total;checked++){
                if(System.nanoTime()>deadline)return;
                // FIX-153: rotate across bounded passes so a slow first lane cannot starve later symbols.
                int lane=Math.floorMod(nextLane++,total);
                String symbol=symbols.get(lane/intervals.size()),interval=intervals.get(lane%intervals.size());
                if(!dispatcher.idle(symbol,interval))continue;
                long lookback=Math.max(180,com.crypto.shared.SharedEventPolicy.closeGraceSeconds(interval)*2L);
                // Index is on open_time: include one full interval before the close-age lookback.
                Instant lowerOpen=now.minusSeconds(intervalSeconds(interval)+lookback);
                var candidates=jdbc.query("SELECT open_time,close_time FROM candle WHERE symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND closed=1 AND close_transition_token IS NOT NULL AND (ingestion_source IS NULL OR ingestion_source<>'BACKFILL') AND open_time>=? AND close_time<=? ORDER BY open_time ASC LIMIT 4",
                    (rs,n)->new CandleClosedEvent(symbol,interval,rs.getTimestamp(1).toInstant(),rs.getTimestamp(2).toInstant()),symbol,interval,Timestamp.from(lowerOpen),Timestamp.from(now));
                for(var event:candidates){
                    String retryKey=key(event);
                    Instant knownAnalysedUntil=analysedUntil.get(retryKey);
                    if(knownAnalysedUntil!=null && knownAnalysedUntil.isAfter(now))continue;
                    Retry retry=retries.get(retryKey);
                    if(retry!=null && retry.nextAttempt().isAfter(now))continue;
                    // A healthy analysis is terminal for detector purposes. Do this
                    // before dispatcher admission so ordinary completed candles never
                    // become retry/expiry noise.
                    if(!missing(event)){retries.remove(retryKey);analysedUntil.put(retryKey,eligibilityEndsAfter(event,lookback));continue;}
                    // No database fence; existing striped candle lock covers only check+handoff.
                    var acquired=coordination.tryLock(symbol,interval,event.openTime());
                    if(acquired.isEmpty())continue;
                    try(var ignored=acquired.get()){
                        if(dispatcher.submitDetected(event,()->missing(event))){
                            // Admission is not completion. Retain bounded state until
                            // durable signal/work evidence appears on a later pass.
                            // Every re-admission consumes the same bounded retry budget;
                            // a repeatedly failing worker must not execute forever.
                            recordAdmission(retryKey,event,now,lookback);
                            break;
                        }
                        defer(retryKey,event,now,"NOT_ADMITTED");
                    }
                }
            }
            for(var iterator=retries.entrySet().iterator();iterator.hasNext();){
                var entry=iterator.next();
                // Retention is strictly after the SQL lower-open-time boundary.
                // Therefore pruning cannot make a still-detectable candle restart
                // with a fresh retry budget.
                if(entry.getValue().retainUntil().isBefore(now)){
                    log.info("[FIX-153][DETECTOR_RETRY_PRUNED] candle={}, attempts={}, reason=NO_LONGER_ELIGIBLE",entry.getKey(),entry.getValue().attempts());
                    iterator.remove();
                }
            }
            analysedUntil.entrySet().removeIf(e->!e.getValue().isAfter(now));
        }catch(RuntimeException failure){LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.count(LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.Event.DETECTOR_FAILED);
            log.warn("[FIX-153][DETECTOR_FAILED] no checkpoint advanced; next bounded pass may retry",failure);}
    }
    private static String key(CandleClosedEvent e){return e.symbol()+"|"+e.intervalCode()+"|"+e.openTime();}
    private void defer(String key,CandleClosedEvent event,Instant now,String reason){
        Retry prior=retries.get(key);int attempts=prior==null?1:prior.attempts()+1;
        long delay=Math.min(60,1L<<Math.min(6,attempts));
        long lookback=Math.max(180,com.crypto.shared.SharedEventPolicy.closeGraceSeconds(event.intervalCode())*2L);
        Instant retryDeadline=event.closeTime().plusSeconds(lookback);
        Instant retainUntil=eligibilityEndsAfter(event,lookback);
        if(attempts>6 || !retryDeadline.isAfter(now)){
            // Keep a terminal marker until this candle has aged out of the SQL
            // lookback. Removing it immediately would restart the retry budget.
            retries.put(key,new Retry(attempts,Instant.MAX,retryDeadline,retainUntil));
            recordExpired(event,reason);
            log.warn("[FIX-153][DETECTOR_GAP_EXPIRED] candle={}, attempts={}, reason={}",key,attempts,reason);return;
        }
        retries.put(key,new Retry(attempts,now.plusSeconds(delay),retryDeadline,retainUntil));
        log.info("[FIX-153][DETECTOR_RETRY_DEFERRED] candle={}, attempts={}, nextUtc={}, reason={}",key,attempts,now.plusSeconds(delay),reason);
    }
    private void recordAdmission(String key,CandleClosedEvent event,Instant now,long lookback){
        Retry prior=retries.get(key);int attempts=prior==null?1:prior.attempts()+1;
        Instant retryDeadline=event.closeTime().plusSeconds(lookback);
        Instant retainUntil=eligibilityEndsAfter(event,lookback);
        if(attempts>6 || !retryDeadline.isAfter(now)){
            // See defer(): this marker is deliberately retained until the query
            // can no longer rediscover this candle.
            retries.put(key,new Retry(attempts,Instant.MAX,retryDeadline,retainUntil));
            recordExpired(event,"ADMISSION_RETRY_LIMIT");
            log.warn("[FIX-153][DETECTOR_GAP_EXPIRED] candle={}, attempts={}, reason=ADMISSION_RETRY_LIMIT",key,attempts);
            return;
        }
        long delay=retryDelaySeconds(attempts);
        retries.put(key,new Retry(attempts,now.plusSeconds(delay),retryDeadline,retainUntil));
        log.info("[FIX-153][DETECTOR_ADMITTED_AWAITING_EVIDENCE] candle={}, attempts={}, nextUtc={}",key,attempts,now.plusSeconds(delay));
    }
    /** Strictly after the candidate predicate: open_time >= now - interval - lookback. */
    private static Instant eligibilityEndsAfter(CandleClosedEvent event,long lookback){
        return event.openTime().plusSeconds(intervalSeconds(event.intervalCode())+lookback+1);
    }
    private static long retryDelaySeconds(int attempts){return Math.min(60,1L<<Math.min(6,attempts));}
    private record Retry(int attempts,Instant nextAttempt,Instant retryDeadline,Instant retainUntil) {}
    private static long intervalSeconds(String interval){
        return switch(interval){case "1m"->60;case "5m"->300;case "15m"->900;case "1h"->3600;case "4h"->14400;case "1d"->86400;
            default->throw new IllegalArgumentException("FIX-153 unsupported detector interval: "+interval);};
    }
    private boolean missing(CandleClosedEvent e){
        if(outcomes!=null && outcomes.exists(e.symbol(),e.intervalCode(),e.openTime()))return false;
        return jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT (EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM trade_signal WHERE symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND candle_open_time=?) OR EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM signal_processing_work WHERE symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND candle_open_time=?))",Integer.class,
            e.symbol(),e.intervalCode(),Timestamp.from(e.openTime()),e.symbol(),e.intervalCode(),Timestamp.from(e.openTime()))==0;
    }
    private void recordExpired(CandleClosedEvent event,String reason){
        if(outcomes!=null)outcomes.record(event.symbol(),event.intervalCode(),event.openTime(),"ANALYSIS_EXPIRED",reason,null,"LOCAL_DETECTOR");
    }

    private SignalDecision appendExternalAdjustment(
            List<DecisionAdjustment> path,
            int sequence,
            String source,
            SignalDecision current,
            SignalDecision serviceDecision,
            boolean entryBefore,
            boolean entryAfter,
            String reason,
            boolean unavailable
    ) {
        SignalDecision after = serviceDecision == null ? current : serviceDecision;
        DecisionAdjustmentType type;
        if (unavailable) {
            type = DecisionAdjustmentType.UNAVAILABLE;
        } else if (after != current || (entryBefore && !entryAfter)) {
            type = entryBefore && !entryAfter ? DecisionAdjustmentType.VETO : DecisionAdjustmentType.DOWNGRADE;
        } else {
            type = DecisionAdjustmentType.PASS;
        }
        path.add(new DecisionAdjustment(
                sequence, source, type, current, after,
                entryBefore, entryAfter, reason
        ));
        return after;
    }

    private int rawConfidence(
            MarketRegimeAssessment regime,
            MarketContextSnapshot context,
            MultiTimeframeConfluenceResult confluence,
            BtcMarketContextResult btc,
            DerivativesPositioningResult derivatives,
            OrderBookLiquidityResult liquidity
    ) {
        double value = regime.confidence() * 0.25;
        value += (context.dataValid() ? 100 : 0) * 0.15;
        value += confluenceConfidence(confluence.status()) * 0.20;
        value += btcConfidence(btc.contextStatus(), btc.stable()) * 0.15;
        value += liquidityConfidence(liquidity.status(), liquidity.observations()) * 0.12;
        value += derivativesConfidence(derivatives) * 0.08;
        value += sentimentConfidence(context) * 0.10;
        int result = (int) Math.round(value);
        return Math.max(0, Math.min(100, result));
    }

    private int confluenceConfidence(ConfluenceStatus status) {
        return switch (status) {
            case STRONG_AGREEMENT -> 100;
            case AGREEMENT -> 90;
            case MIXED -> 60;
            case CONFLICT -> 30;
            case STRONG_CONFLICT -> 10;
            case UNAVAILABLE -> 40;
        };
    }

    private int btcConfidence(BtcContextStatus status, boolean stable) {
        int base = switch (status) {
            case CONFIRMED -> 95;
            case NOT_APPLICABLE -> 85;
            case NEUTRAL -> 75;
            case LEARNING -> 50;
            case CONFLICT -> 30;
            case STRONG_CONFLICT -> 10;
            case UNAVAILABLE -> 40;
            case STALE_CONTEXT -> 0;
        };
        return stable ? base : Math.max(0, base - 10);
    }

    private int liquidityConfidence(LiquidityContextStatus status, int observations) {
        int base = switch (status) {
            case BULLISH_SUPPORT -> 95;
            case BALANCED, DISABLED -> 85;
            case LEARNING -> 55;
            case INSUFFICIENT_DATA_HOLD -> 25;
            case UNAVAILABLE -> 40;
            case BEARISH_PRESSURE, STOP_EXPOSED -> 35;
            case TARGET_BLOCKED, THIN_LIQUIDITY -> 15;
            case WALL_WEAKENING -> 65;
        };
        if (observations <= 0 && status != LiquidityContextStatus.DISABLED) {
            return Math.min(base, 40);
        }
        return base;
    }

    private int derivativesConfidence(DerivativesPositioningResult result) {
        int base = switch (result.status()) {
            case FRESH_LONG_BUILDUP, FRESH_SHORT_BUILDUP -> 95;
            case HEALTHY_BULLISH, HEALTHY_BEARISH, BALANCED, NOT_APPLICABLE -> 80;
            case SHORT_COVERING, LONG_LIQUIDATION, LOW_CONVICTION -> 55;
            case LONGS_CROWDED, SHORTS_CROWDED -> 25;
            case LEARNING -> 50;
            case UNAVAILABLE -> 40;
        };
        return Math.max(0, Math.min(100, base + result.confidenceAdjustment()));
    }

            return ValidationResult.reject("NOT_SELL", "The 1m decision is not SELL/STRONG_SELL.");
        }
        return ValidationResult.allow(100, "BASE_VALID", "Base execution signal is valid.");
    }

    private TradeSignal latestAtOrBefore(TradeSignal signal, String interval) {
        return latestSignal(signal.getSymbol(), interval, signal.getGeneratedAt()).orElse(null);
    }

    private java.util.Optional<TradeSignal> previousSignal(String symbol, String interval, Instant reference) {
        if (replayScope != null && replayScope.active()) return replayScope.previousBefore(symbol, interval, reference);
        return signalRepository.findTopBySymbolAndIntervalAndGeneratedAtLessThanOrderByGeneratedAtDesc(symbol, interval, reference);
    }

    private java.util.Optional<TradeSignal> latestSignal(String symbol, String interval, Instant reference) {
        if (replayScope != null && replayScope.active()) return replayScope.latestAtOrBefore(symbol, interval, reference);
        return signalRepository.findTopBySymbolAndIntervalAndGeneratedAtLessThanEqualOrderByGeneratedAtDesc(symbol, interval, reference);
    }

    private boolean isFresh(TradeSignal signal, Instant reference, Duration maximumAge) {
        return signal != null
                && signal.getGeneratedAt() != null
                && !signal.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(reference)
                && !signal.getGeneratedAt().isBefore(reference.minus(maximumAge));
    }

    private boolean isBullish(SignalDecision decision) {
        return decision == SignalDecision.BUY || decision == SignalDecision.STRONG_BUY;
    }

    private boolean isBearish(SignalDecision decision) {
        return decision == SignalDecision.SELL || decision == SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL;
    }

    private boolean isWatchOrNeutral(SignalDecision decision) {
        return decision == SignalDecision.WATCH || decision == SignalDecision.NEUTRAL;
    }

    private WalletSettings settings() {
        return settingsRepository.findById(1L)
                .orElseGet(() -> WalletSettings.builder()
                        .id(1L)
                        .executionProfile(ExecutionProfile.BALANCED.name())
                        .requireNewBuyTransition(true)
                        .dynamicProfitLockEnabled(true)
                        .profitLockActivationPercent(BigDecimal.valueOf(70))
                        .profitLockInitialPercent(BigDecimal.valueOf(40))
                        .profitLockTrailStepPercent(BigDecimal.valueOf(10))
                        .build());
    }

    private ValidationResult cap(ValidationResult result, HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Assessment assessment) {
        if (!result.allowed() || assessment.maxInitialPercent()==null) return result;
        int capped=Math.min(result.positionPercent(),assessment.maxInitialPercent());
        if (capped != result.positionPercent() && fix154Metrics != null) fix154Metrics.softCappedEntry();
        return capped==result.positionPercent()?result:ValidationResult.allow(capped,result.code(),result.explanation()+" FIX-154 soft-HTF cap="+capped+"%.");
    }

    /** Used only after progressive raw-HTF checks have already failed; never changes an add decision. */
    public void recordProgressiveAssessmentIfSoftBlocked(TradeSignal referenceSignal) {
        if (referenceSignal == null || referenceSignal.getGeneratedAt() == null) return;
        TradeSignal five = latestAtOrBefore(referenceSignal, CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL);
        TradeSignal one = latestAtOrBefore(referenceSignal, TREND_INTERVAL);
        if (!isFresh(five, referenceSignal.getGeneratedAt(), FIVE_MINUTE_MAX_AGE)
                || !isFresh(one, referenceSignal.getGeneratedAt(), ONE_HOUR_MAX_AGE)) return;
        HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Assessment assessment = htfAssessment.assess(five, true, one, true);
        if (assessment.band() == HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Band.SOFT_CONFLICT) {
            if (fix154Metrics != null) fix154Metrics.softAddBlocked();
            audit(referenceSignal, "PROGRESSIVE_ADD", assessment, "SOFT_HTF_ADD_BLOCKED",
                    "Existing raw 1h progressive-add guard blocked this add; FIX-154 audit only.");
        }
    }

    private void audit(TradeSignal referenceSignal, String path,
                       HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Assessment assessment, String finalResult, String reason) {
        if (htfAuditStore == null) return;
        try {
            htfAuditStore.record(referenceSignal, path, assessment, finalResult, reason);
        } catch (RuntimeException auditFailure) {
            if (fix154Metrics != null) fix154Metrics.auditWriteFailed();
            log.warn("FIX-154 audit handoff failed; BUY decision remains unchanged: path={}, signalId={}",
                    path, referenceSignal == null ? null : referenceSignal.getId(), auditFailure);
        }

    public Assessment evaluate(TradeSignal currentSignal) {
        if (currentSignal == null || currentSignal.getGeneratedAt() == null) {
            return Assessment.reject("INVALID_SIGNAL", "Current execution signal is missing.");
        }
        if (!EXECUTION_INTERVAL.equals(currentSignal.getInterval()) || !isBullish(currentSignal.getDecision())) {
            return Assessment.reject("NOT_1M_BUY", "Opportunity consolidation only evaluates fresh 1m BUY/STRONG_BUY signals.");
        }

        TradeSignal fiveMinute = latestAtOrBefore(currentSignal, CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL);
        TradeSignal oneHour = latestAtOrBefore(currentSignal, TREND_INTERVAL);

        if (!isFresh(fiveMinute, currentSignal.getGeneratedAt(), FIVE_MINUTE_MAX_AGE)) {
            return Assessment.reject("MISSING_5M_CONTEXT", "No fresh 5m context is available for consolidation.");
        }
        if (!isFresh(oneHour, currentSignal.getGeneratedAt(), ONE_HOUR_MAX_AGE)) {
            return Assessment.reject("MISSING_1H_CONTEXT", "No fresh 1h context is available for consolidation.");
        }
        HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Assessment htf=htfAssessment.assess(fiveMinute,true,oneHour,true);
        if(!htf.allowed()) {
            audit(currentSignal, htf, htf.code(), "FIX-154 higher-timeframe assessment rejected consolidation.");
            return Assessment.reject(htf.code(), "FIX-154 higher-timeframe assessment rejected consolidation.");
        }
        SignalDecision fiveDecision=htf.effectiveFiveMinute();
        SignalDecision oneDecision=htf.effectiveOneHour();

        List<TradeSignal> recent = replayScope != null && replayScope.active()
                ? replayScope.recent(currentSignal.getSymbol(), EXECUTION_INTERVAL, currentSignal.getGeneratedAt(), 20)
                : signalRepository.findTop20BySymbolAndIntervalOrderByGeneratedAtDesc(currentSignal.getSymbol(), EXECUTION_INTERVAL);
        List<TradeSignal> consecutive = consecutiveFreshBuys(recent, currentSignal.getGeneratedAt());

        int count = consecutive.size();
        if (count < MIN_CONSECUTIVE_BUYS) {
            Assessment result = Assessment.building(
                    count,
                    averageScore(consecutive),
                    averageConfidence(consecutive),
                    firstScore(consecutive),
package com.crypto.service;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;

import com.crypto.domain.SignalDecision;
import com.crypto.domain.TradeSignal;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

class HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentServiceTest {
    private final HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService service = new HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService();

    @Test void hardTrustedBearishOneHourVetoesWhenFiveMinuteIsNotBearish() {
        var result = service.assess(signal(SignalDecision.BUY, true, 80), true,
                signal(SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL, true, 70), true);
        assertFalse(result.allowed());
        assertEquals("HTF_TRUSTED_BEARISH_VETO", result.code());
    }

    @Test void softTrustedBearishUsesDetachedNeutralContextAndCapsSize() {
        TradeSignal one = signal(SignalDecision.SELL, true, 80);
        var result = service.assess(signal(SignalDecision.BUY, true, 80), true, one, true);
        assertTrue(result.allowed());
        assertEquals(SignalDecision.NEUTRAL, result.effectiveOneHour());
        assertEquals(25, result.maxInitialPercent());
        assertEquals(SignalDecision.SELL, one.getDecision());
    }

    @Test void bearishFiveMinuteKeepsLegacyReasonBeforeHardOneHour() {
        var result = service.assess(signal(SignalDecision.SELL, true, 80), true,
                signal(SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL, true, 80), true);
        assertEquals("5M_BEARISH_VETO", result.code());
        assertTrue(result.hardConfirmationPresent());
    }

    @Test void unknownBearishOneHourFailsClosed() {
        var result = service.assess(signal(SignalDecision.BUY, true, 80), true,
                signal(SignalDecision.SELL, false, 80), true);
        assertEquals("1H_BEARISH_VETO", result.code());
    }

    @Test void untrustedNonBearishOneHourRetainsItsRawAuthority() {
        var result = service.assess(signal(SignalDecision.BUY, true, 80), true,
                signal(SignalDecision.BUY, false, 80), true);
        assertTrue(result.allowed());
        assertEquals(SignalDecision.BUY, result.effectiveOneHour());
        assertEquals(HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Band.NOT_TRUSTED, result.band());
    }

    @Test void strongSellAtSixtyNineIsSoftButAtSeventyIsHard() {
        var soft = service.assess(signal(SignalDecision.BUY, true, 80), true,
                signal(SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL, true, 69), true);
        var hard = service.assess(signal(SignalDecision.BUY, true, 80), true,
                signal(SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL, true, 70), true);
        assertTrue(soft.allowed());
        assertEquals(HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Band.SOFT_CONFLICT, soft.band());
        assertEquals(25, soft.maxInitialPercent());
        assertFalse(hard.allowed());
        assertEquals("HTF_TRUSTED_BEARISH_VETO", hard.code());
    }

    @Test void fiveMinuteQualityDoesNotChangeTheLegacyBearishVeto() {
        TradeSignal five = signal(SignalDecision.SELL, false, 80);
        var result = service.assess(five, true, signal(SignalDecision.BUY, true, 80), true);
        assertEquals("5M_BEARISH_VETO", result.code());
    }

    @Test void bnb150aPersistedUnknownBearishContextKeepsLegacyOneHourVeto() {
        var result = service.assess(signal(SignalDecision.BUY, true, 80), true,
                signal(SignalDecision.SELL, false, 70), true);
        assertFalse(result.allowed());
        assertEquals("1H_BEARISH_VETO", result.code());
    }

    @Test void bnb150bReplayRegeneratedQualityEvidenceMakesTheSameBearishRowSoft() {
        var result = service.assess(signal(SignalDecision.BUY, true, 80), true,
                signal(SignalDecision.SELL, true, 70), true);
        assertTrue(result.allowed());
        assertEquals(HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Band.SOFT_CONFLICT, result.band());
        assertEquals(25, result.maxInitialPercent());
        // Risk/reward is intentionally evaluated downstream; FIX-154 does not mask it.
    }

    private TradeSignal signal(SignalDecision decision, boolean quality, int confidence) {
        TradeSignal signal = new TradeSignal();
        signal.setDecision(decision); signal.setDataQualityValid(quality); signal.setConfidenceScore(confidence);
        return signal;
    }

## FINDINGS

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** `Perform` identifies no artifact, claim, or acceptance criterion to approve or reject.

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** Production’s close-event worker enforces the 210-candle/gap/OHLC quality gate; fresh Replay does not call that gate. Replay therefore is not production-parity evidence for windows where Production would reject analysis for candle quality.

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** Replay-generated signals are unsaved and retain `id=null`. Its consumed-entry ledger ignores null IDs, so `BUY_CONTINUATION` cannot identify a prior replay BUY as consumed. Claude’s corrected B2 finding is supported.

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** FIX-154 compares its threshold against `confidenceScore`, which is the effective score and is capped at 49 when entry is blocked. Raw confidence is separately persisted but unused by the FIX-154 assessment. Claude’s B3 mechanics are supported.

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** V109 supplies Replay-table columns already mapped by reflection from `TradeSignal`. A database migrated through V97–V108 but not V109 would fail Replay persistence for those mapped fields. Whether any such database or Replay run exists is unproven.

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** The committed configuration enables reconciliation, while architecture documentation says it must remain disabled pending listed release gates. This is a documentation/configuration contradiction; it does not establish that the observed runtime is Production.

## OBJECTIONS

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** “Replay would produce signals and possibly trades for every candle” is not proven without executing a bounded Replay; snapshot failures and execution gates remain possible.

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** “Every new signal is data-quality-valid” is too broad. `MarketContextService` has a weaker EMA/price predicate, but this does not prove every signal path reaches it with `true`.

- **[AGENT INFERENCE]** The confidence-70 split cannot validate a trading policy or threshold. The cited count is in-sample, lacks outcomes and cost/slippage treatment, and does not address correlated observations or false-veto rate.

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** The worktree is extensively modified and untracked, so there is no immutable source baseline for a broad approval.

## UNKNOWNS

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** The intended review subject for “Perform.”
- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** Actual Flyway history, including whether V109 is applied.
- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** The effective configuration and deployment identity behind local logs.
- **[AGENT INFERENCE]** Frequency and financial impact of the identified Replay divergences require controlled, non-Production validation.
